## Partner dossiers

### 1. Agency Toolchest

- **Tier:** Platinum
- **Website:** https://agencytoolchest.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/agency-toolchest.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `agency-toolchest`
- **Category:** Insurance agency KPI tracking, sales gamification and coaching software
- **Also known as:** Agency Tool Chest, Agency Tool Chest, LLC, AgencyToolChest, ATC
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Todd Mclain
- Vimeo id: 1222089520
- Contact: Book a demo → https://agencytoolchest.com
- Shown as: agencytoolchest.com
- Note: Todd shared Agency Toolchest as the best way to connect and book a demo.

**What we know (22 researched facts)**

- **entity** — The company is Agency Tool Chest, LLC, stated in its Terms of Service to be a Delaware corporation with a principal place of business at 1202 Ridge Crest Rd, Mansfield, Texas 76063. _(public_company_claim)_
- **positioning** — Agency Toolchest positions itself as a KPI and reporting layer for insurance agencies that combines sales gamification with targeted coaching, summarised on the site as gamify goals, guide greatness, glean growth. _(public_company_claim)_
- **chrome_extension** — The product is delivered as a Chrome extension toolbar plus a web dashboard. Staff enter quote or policy information such as premium and quote type from any page and send it to the web application with one click. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dashboard** — The dashboard compiles and analyses the organisation's quotes data, showing rankings and both individual and team performance against goals. _(public_company_claim)_
- **gamification** — Gamification features include gamified targets, celebratory alerts, staff rankings and interoffice messaging with texts and GIFs inside the extension. _(public_company_claim)_
- **goals** — Owners can set and oversee individual staff goals and activity as well as agency-wide goals, and retrieve detailed reports on quotes and sales. _(public_company_claim)_
- **marketing_tracking** — The platform tracks lead sources, identifies which lead sources are most effective, and reports best-selling areas by zip code. _(public_company_claim)_
- **coaching_by_lead_source** — The site states coaching can be tailored based on the source of the lead, and that the dashboard identifies which employees are performing best and which need more attention. _(public_company_claim)_
- **bundling** — The annual plan includes tracking the success rate of bundling efforts. _(public_company_claim)_
- **coming_soon** — Cross-selling tracking and zip code competitiveness tracking are both listed on the pricing plan as COMING SOON rather than as shipped features. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pricing** — The site publishes one plan: an Annual plan at $3000 per year. No other plan or per-seat price is shown on the pages observed. _(public_company_claim)_
- **onboarding_owner** — Onboarding for an owner is to register the organisation, invite team members, and assign roles that match their responsibilities. _(public_company_claim)_
- **onboarding_staff** — A staff member must first be invited by an organisation owner, then pin the extension, toggle the toolbar on, add a household with quotes and sales, and follow results in the dashboard and extension chat. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support** — Support is offered by email at support@agencytoolchest.com, by phone at 1-817-973-5485, and through extension chat for registered users. _(public_company_claim)_
- **cws_listing** — The Chrome Web Store listing is titled Agency Toolchest, categorised under Workflow & Planning, developer Agency Tool Chest at a Mansfield, TX address, version 2.5.5 updated August 26, 2026. _(public_company_claim)_
- **cws_scale** — The Chrome Web Store listing showed 326 users and a 5.0 average from 3 ratings when observed. This is a store metric on one listing, not a count of agencies or seats, and it is undated as a customer figure. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **data_disclosure** — The extension discloses that it handles personally identifiable information, user activity and website content, and declares the data is not sold to third parties outside approved use cases and is not used to determine creditworthiness. _(public_company_claim)_
- **services_definition** — The Terms of Service define the Services broadly as an online lead management platform plus telecommunications, billing, data analysis, training, custom development and related third-party services. _(public_company_claim)_
- **privacy_sources** — The privacy notice states the company may collect information from insurance providers, marketing partners, social media platforms and other outside sources, and that it does not process sensitive personal information. _(public_company_claim)_
- **demo_route** — The Schedule a Demo call to action on the home page routes to an external Calendly booking page under the handle quotingedge rather than to an on-site form. _(public_company_claim)_
- **testimonial_revenue** — A published testimonial from Marcia Hill, Agency Manager, claims that dashboard data and rankings let her spot problems in her agency and that revenue then went up insanely. This is an unverified customer outcome claim. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **copyright** — The site footer carries a 2024 to 2026 copyright for Agency Tool Chest, LLC. _(public_company_claim)_

**Sources**

- Agency Toolchest — Home — https://agencytoolchest.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Agency Toolchest — Quick Guide — https://agencytoolchest.com/quick-guide (seen 2026-09-09)
- Agency Tool Chest, LLC — Terms of Service — https://agencytoolchest.com/terms (seen 2026-09-09)
- Agency Tool Chest, LLC — Privacy Policy — https://agencytoolchest.com/privacy (seen 2026-09-09)
- Agency Toolchest — Chrome Web Store listing — https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/agency-toolchest/anoincpioepaahlggoeiffopdcjjhfjo (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| The Sales Sequence | primary | Accountability | Quote and sale entry at the point of work, individual and agency goals, rankings and detailed quote reports give a producer seat a visible activity floor and a scoreboard. It measures the sequence; it does not define what the sequence is or who sits in the seat. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Position Definition & Focus % | Bundling success tracking touches the service and retention motion, and cross-sell tracking is announced but not shipped. Conditional because the product measures new business activity rather than service delivery or retention. |
| The Operating System | supporting_direct | The Team Leads | Roles, invitations and an agency-wide goal view give a team lead one place to see who is doing what. The operating standard itself, and the decision rights behind it, stay with the owner. |
| Commitment to Training | supporting_direct | Assess the Training | The platform tells an owner who is performing and who needs attention, which is the assessment half of this session. It supplies no curriculum, no scored gate and no training content. |
| Making It Rain | supporting_direct | The Opportunity Distribution | Lead source tracking, best-source identification and zip code reporting make where opportunities came from legible, which is the reporting half of this session. Zip code competitiveness is still listed as coming soon. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime, regardless of who is in the seat. A vendor's commercial context has no place shaping a member's personal work. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime, regardless of who is in the seat. A vendor's commercial context has no place shaping a member's personal work. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime, regardless of who is in the seat. A vendor's commercial context has no place shaping a member's personal work. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Write the daily activity number for one producer seat, then decide which of those numbers you would actually put on a leaderboard.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Quotes and sales logged per producer per day against the goal you set.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Decide the one service moment you would measure, and check whether anything in your current stack actually records it.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Bundled items per new household, tracked weekly.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name the one number your team lead should own, and decide who sees it daily.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the agency-wide goal is reviewed on a set cadence without the owner initiating it.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick the one metric that would tell you a producer needs coaching this week, and decide who reviews it.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Number of coaching conversations triggered by a dashboard signal rather than by a gut feel.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: List every lead source you paid for last month and write what each one produced in quotes and sales.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Quotes and sales attributed per lead source, month over month.

**On these resource pages**

- **Formula Sales Sequence Resources** (/resources/sales-sequence)
  - Helps with: Sales performance visibility, scoreboards, accountability and targeted coaching.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves knowing whether producers are executing the standard and where coaching is needed.
- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Agency performance visibility, scoreboards, KPIs and accountability reporting.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves seeing what the team is doing without asking each person.

**Open questions**

- Whether the published $3000 per year Annual plan is per agency or per seat, and whether a monthly option exists. Only the single annual figure is published.
- Any integration with agency management systems, carrier raters or comparative quoting tools. The extension captures data by hand from whatever page the user is on; no integration list is published.
- Whether Cross-selling tracking and Zip code competitiveness tracking have shipped since they were labelled COMING SOON on the pricing plan.
- Company leadership, team size, founding date and ownership. No about or team page exists on the site.
- The relationship between Agency Tool Chest, LLC and the quotingedge Calendly handle that the Schedule a Demo button routes to.
- Customer count. The Chrome Web Store listing showed 326 users of that extension, which is not the same as agencies, seats or active customers, and is undated as a customer figure.
- Any security, uptime, SOC 2 or data residency documentation. None is published on the pages observed.
- Whether the product supports captive, independent or both agency models, and which carriers or systems its users typically quote in.
- Any published outcome, retention or production figures. The only results language on the site is an unverified customer testimonial.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: awaiting_account_and_organization
- note: Research approved for parsing only. Bind the exact existing partner organization before any write, then separately authorize the Firebase Functions release. Book mapping is editorial analysis, not partner-approved placement.

### 2. MediaAlpha

- **Tier:** Platinum
- **Website:** https://mediaalpha.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/mediaalpha.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `mediaalpha`
- **Category:** Two-sided programmatic insurance customer acquisition marketplace selling clicks, leads and calls to carriers and agents
- **Also known as:** MediaAlpha for Agents, Quotelab, LLC, MediaAlpha Exchange, MAX
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Tigran Mekikian
- Vimeo id: 1223154108
- Contact: Email the team → mailto:agentsupport@mediaalpha.com,agentsales@mediaalpha.com
- Shown as: agentsupport@mediaalpha.com · agentsales@mediaalpha.com
- Note: Tigran shared these team inboxes as the best way for new and existing agents to connect with MediaAlpha.

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **what** — MediaAlpha runs a two-sided programmatic customer acquisition marketplace for insurance, with three participant types: advertisers who buy, publishers who supply traffic, and agents who buy leads. Its stated mission is to connect every insurance carrier with in-market shoppers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **legal_entity** — The operating entity behind the agent product is Quotelab, LLC, doing business as MediaAlpha, per the consent language on the agent signup form. _(public_company_claim)_
- **company_profile** — MediaAlpha was co-founded by Steve Yi, Chief Executive Officer, and Eugene Nonko, Chief Architect. It publishes offices in Los Angeles, Bellevue Washington, Tempe Arizona and Taipei, and maintains an investor relations site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agents_product** — MediaAlpha for Agents is a lead-buying platform where property and casualty insurance agents set up an account and buy leads, built on the same principles as the carrier marketplace. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lead_types** — The agent product currently offers auto, home and life leads. MediaAlpha states it may offer other lead products in the future. _(public_company_claim)_
- **buying_modes** — There are two ways an agent buys. Automatic Lead Delivery is a standing campaign where the agent sets filters, the price they are willing to pay per lead and a daily lead cap, and is notified each time they win a lead. Browse and buy lets the agent purchase individual leads at a fixed price. _(public_company_claim)_
- **targeting_controls** — Agents filter by geography and by consumer type such as premium, preferred, standard and non-standard, and set their own delivery schedule and daily limits so leads arrive only when someone is available to work them. Filters can be changed at any time. _(public_company_claim)_
- **browse_transparency** — In browse and buy, MediaAlpha states the agent can see how many times each lead has already been sold, to which carriers, and which carrier has already contacted the consumer, before deciding to purchase. _(public_company_claim)_
- **shared_lead_rules** — Unless a lead is identified as exclusive, MediaAlpha sells shared leads to agents and to corporate carriers, with each shared lead sold to no more than four buyers, and no lead sold to more than one agent affiliated with a particular captive carrier. _(public_company_claim)_
- **commercial_terms** — MediaAlpha states there is no minimum spend and no minimum contract term for agents. The minimum deposit is $250, and MediaAlpha reserves the right to introduce programs requiring a higher minimum deposit. _(public_company_claim)_
- **payment_mechanics** — Accounts are created by the Client Success Team with a credit card and a prepaid deposit. Leads are bought against that deposit, an auto-refill feature adds funds, and an account without sufficient funds cannot bid on or purchase new leads. _(public_company_claim)_
- **return_policy** — Bad leads can be returned with a Return button on the lead. Requests submitted within 72 hours of delivery are instantly approved, requests can be made for up to 7 days, and up to 20 percent of leads received in a month may be returned, with anything beyond 20 percent at MediaAlpha's discretion and the ability to pause returns without notice. _(public_company_claim)_
- **bad_lead_definition** — MediaAlpha defines a returnable bad lead as one of four things: invalid contact information where both phone and email are proven invalid, fictitious lead information such as a fake name, a filter criteria error where the lead does not match account settings, or a duplicate received from MediaAlpha within the last 30 days. A returned lead must not be used and all copies must be deleted. _(public_company_claim)_
- **delivery_and_integrations** — Leads are notified by email, optionally by text message, or posted into the agent's lead management system. MediaAlpha states dozens of integrations are available and names Ricochet360, AgencyZoom, Allstate Lead Manager and APEX. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pause_and_cancel** — Accounts can be paused at any time from Account Setup, which stops automated campaigns while leaving browse and buy available. On cancellation, available funds are refunded excluding matching or referral bonuses. Additional team members are added by contacting the Client Success Team. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_volume_guarantee** — MediaAlpha states plainly that because it is a dynamic marketplace with a right-price bidding model, it cannot guarantee a certain number of leads per day. Volume is changed by adjusting lead price or filters, and the daily cap limits the upside deliberately. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sourcing** — Inventory is sourced from MediaAlpha's owned and operated insurance properties and from trusted publishing partners, with supply policies stated to enforce quality across the marketplace. MediaAlpha partners with third-party vendors to verify lead data before selling. _(public_company_claim)_
- **consent** — MediaAlpha states all consumers consent to be contacted by phone, text and email by its agent partners when they submit the quote form, and points to its Agent Terms and Privacy Policy. Agents remain responsible for their own compliance posture. _(public_company_claim)_
- **subsidy_and_referral** — MediaAlpha states it participates in certain carrier subsidy programs for agent leads, and runs a referral program giving credit for each referred agent who deposits and starts buying. Details on both are given by a client success manager rather than published. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support** — Agent onboarding runs through agentleads@mediaalpha.com or the site form, and ongoing account support through agentsupport@mediaalpha.com. MediaAlpha describes a United States based Client Success Team with an average tenure of five or more years. _(public_company_claim)_
- **advertiser_side** — On the advertiser side MediaAlpha sells real-time algorithmic bidding for carriers, with every click priced dynamically from live data, granular source-level transparency and real-time reporting, robust APIs for integration, a fully managed option, and SOC 2 Type II compliance. _(public_company_claim)_
- **targeting_breadth** — MediaAlpha states targeting is available across 35 or more consumer attributes, and that buyers can filter by geography, line of business and device type. Traffic reaches the marketplace from brand, metasearch and comparison search websites. _(public_company_claim)_
- **publisher_side** — Publishers monetise clicks, leads and calls through one platform, including carriers monetising non-converting consumers. MediaAlpha states advertisers bid on publisher traffic channels by name so the publisher can see which advertiser performs best and at what price. _(public_company_claim)_
- **scale_claims** — Undated marketing figures published across the site include over $2B in 2025 platform spend, 16 of the top 20 US auto and P&C carriers, 11M+ consumer referrals per month and 130M+ to 140M+ annually, 700+ demand partners, 350+ supply partners, 1,000 to 1,100+ active partners, 5K+ active carriers and agents, 1M+ leads sold to agents per month, 5M+ impressions per day and 99.9% uptime. None were independently verified and several are stated inconsistently between pages. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **quality_positioning** — MediaAlpha describes itself as the leader in insurance customer acquisition and the industry's leading programmatic customer acquisition platform, and states its leads are never sold to more than one agent per carrier. These are the company's own positioning claims and were not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Insurance Customer Acquisition Platform — MediaAlpha — https://mediaalpha.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- High-Quality Insurance Leads for Agents — MediaAlpha — https://mediaalpha.com/agents/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Insurance Advertising Solutions for Marketers — MediaAlpha — https://mediaalpha.com/advertisers/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Publisher Monetization Platform — MediaAlpha — https://mediaalpha.com/publishers/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Why MediaAlpha — Transparent Customer Acquisition — https://mediaalpha.com/why-mediaalpha/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- About MediaAlpha — Customer Acquisition Technology — https://mediaalpha.com/about/ (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Making It Rain | primary | The Opportunity Distribution | This is a paid opportunity channel with real controls: bid, filters, daily cap, schedule, and visibility into how many buyers already have the lead. That makes spend legible enough to compare against your other channels. It is a purchase, not a strategy — the marketplace explicitly will not guarantee volume, so the decision about how much of your distribution should be bought stays with the owner. |
| The Sales Sequence | supporting_direct | Accountability | A purchased lead has a clock on it and a cost attached, which is the sharpest accountability data a sales manager can get. Delivery into the agency's lead management system, schedule controls and a 72-hour return window all put a measurable frame around producer follow-up. The sequence itself, and the consequence when it is not run, still belong to the agency. |
| The Operating System | supporting_direct | Your Agency Bible | Deposits, auto-refill, a 20 percent monthly return ceiling and a 72-hour instant-approval window are operating rules with money attached, and they fail quietly when nobody owns them. Writing down who reviews returns, who watches the balance and who may pause the account is a genuine Agency Bible entry. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Elite Onboarding Experience | Conditional. A purchased shopper is not a served client, and up to four buyers may be calling the same person. Anything worth calling a service standard starts after the sale, and nothing in the marketplace does that work. The consent posture and the integration path are the only pieces that touch onboarding at all. |
| Commitment to Training | conditional | The GOATs Train Every Day | MediaAlpha provides client success guidance on lead buying strategy, which is coaching for the buyer rather than training for the team. Conditional because there is no curriculum, no scoring and no completion evidence, and a shared lead demands a specific kind of call skill that the agency has to build itself. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Write what each of your channels produced last month in leads, quotes and premium, then decide what a purchased lead is worth to you before you set a bid.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Cost per bound policy by channel, purchased leads compared against every other source.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Set the standard for speed to first contact on a paid lead, then decide what happens when it is missed twice.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Minutes to first contact on purchased leads, and contact rate within the return window.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name who owns the lead account: who sets the bid, who submits returns, who is allowed to pause it. Write it down today.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Return rate against the 20 percent ceiling, and whether returns are submitted inside 72 hours.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Write what happens in the first 30 days after a purchased lead becomes a policyholder, and who owns each step.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Retention at first renewal on purchased-lead business compared with referral business.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Role play the opening 20 seconds of a call to a shopper who has already spoken to two other agents today.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Contact-to-quote rate on purchased leads by producer.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Insurance customer acquisition through a marketplace of shoppers, clicks and calls.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves adding inbound volume from consumers already shopping for insurance.

**Open questions**

- Lead prices. MediaAlpha publishes a bidding model and a $250 minimum deposit but no cost per lead for any product, geography or consumer type.
- Which carrier subsidy programs MediaAlpha participates in, and what the subsidy is worth. The site routes this to a client success manager.
- The referral program's actual credit amount and its terms.
- The full list of lead management system integrations. Four are named and the rest are described only as dozens.
- How exclusive leads are priced, how often they are available, and whether an agent can buy exclusives alone.
- What the third-party verification vendors check, and what proportion of leads fail verification.
- Actual close rates, cost per policy or return on ad spend for any agent. None are published and none were asserted.
- Whether the agent platform and the carrier marketplace draw from the same inventory pool at the same time, and how an agent's bid competes with a carrier's.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_representative_checked
- disclosure: Research compiled from MediaAlpha's public marketing and agent-facing website only, including its published agent FAQ. No account, contract, rate card or platform login was seen, and no MediaAlpha representative reviewed this record.
- note: Book mapping is editorial analysis, not partner-approved placement. Bind an existing organization and authorize release separately before this is loaded at runtime.

### 3. SecureEVAs

- **Tier:** Platinum
- **Website:** https://secureevas.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/secure-evas.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `secure-evas`
- **Category:** Insurance-trained executive virtual assistants (remote agency admin and service support)
- **Also known as:** Secure EVA, SecureEVA, Secure EVAs, secureevas.com
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Chris Cole
- Vimeo id: 1225291754
- Contact: Call Chris → tel:+15713732206
- Shown as: 571-373-2206
- Note: Chris shared his phone number as the best way to connect with Secure EVAS.

**What we know (23 researched facts)**

- **what_it_is** — SecureEVAs places Executive Virtual Assistants (EVAs) who are pre-trained for insurance work, positioned as remote admin for insurance agents rather than general-purpose virtual assistants. The company describes itself as a secure support system rather than a staffing company. _(public_company_claim)_
- **founder_origin** — The company names Chris Cole as founder and states its founders came out of building and operating a large insurance agency, where they judged traditional staffing hard to sustain at scale and general outsourcing unsafe for compliance-heavy work. _(public_company_claim)_
- **task_scope** — Published task scope is policy service and client support, claims intake and follow-up, renewals and COIs, plus service requests and general agency admin. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pre_trained** — The company states EVAs arrive already trained rather than being taught on the job: insurance workflow familiarity, documentation standards, compliance-focused operations, communication expectations and security procedures are stated to be in place before day one. _(public_company_claim)_
- **domain_knowledge** — EVAs are described as familiar with carriers, AMS tools, policy lifecycles and service SLAs before placement. _(public_company_claim)_
- **security_claims** — SecureEVAs publicly claims SOC 2 Type 2 certification with independently audited controls across security, availability and confidentiality, and states it is HIPAA compliant. It also cites daily compliance audits, continuous monitoring, secure virtual environments, structured access controls, and device-level policies including encrypted devices. _(public_company_claim)_
- **security_positioning** — Security is positioned as the differentiator, not a feature: the company frames the choice as proof versus trust — 'not a trust me setup' — and argues that agencies hesitate to outsource because policyholder data and the agency's reputation are at stake. _(public_company_claim)_
- **process** — The published onboarding is three steps, named The Secure Support Process: share your needs on a stated 20-minute discovery call mapping workflows, tools, pain points, compliance concerns and goals; get matched; then delegate through structured onboarding with secure systems installed and ongoing coaching. _(public_company_claim)_
- **candidate_choice** — The company states it presents three vetted EVA candidates aligned to the agency, that the agency interviews and chooses, and that it never does random placement. Matching criteria named are communication style, operational fit, personality fit, workflow alignment and long-term compatibility. _(public_company_claim)_
- **billing_gate** — SecureEVAs states the agency is not billed until onboarding is complete, secure systems are in place and the assistant is ready to deliver. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dedicated_assistant** — The FAQ states the agency is matched with one EVA who becomes embedded in its operations — not a rotating team and not a shared resource. _(public_company_claim)_
- **customization** — Support is stated to be tailored to the agency's own tools, SOPs, client touchpoints and voice rather than delivered from a fixed vendor process. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sop_continuity** — The company publishes a continuity model built around documentation: SOP documentation support so processes do not live only in one person's head, workflow preservation, operational continuity if a person leaves, structured transition and handoff support, and knowledge retention with the agency. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ongoing_coaching** — Ongoing coaching after placement is stated for both the EVA and the agency owner, positioned as a long-term partnership rather than a one-time placement. _(public_company_claim)_
- **role_split** — Placement is stated to be by role — back office or client-facing — rather than a single generic assistant profile. _(public_company_claim)_
- **segment** — The stated customer range runs from independent agencies to national carriers, with the pitch framed around three pain points: time, money and trust. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact** — Published contact points are (866) 208-6996 and sales@secureevas.com, with the company described as United States based with remote teams. The entry point offered is a discovery call. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contract_terms_claim** — The site states 'no long contracts, no surprises', but publishes no contract length, notice period, hours commitment or rate anywhere on the pages observed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **outcome_stats** — Undated outcome figures on the homepage: 18 hours per week saved on admin in the first 30 days for an average agency, roughly 60% lower cost than in-house admin, and 96% of compliance checks clean. No methodology, sample or period is stated. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **retention_conflict** — Retention is published inconsistently: the homepage states 99% retention of client accounts while the About page states a 97% client retention rate. Both are undated and the two figures were observed on the same day. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **scale_claims** — The About page claims 300+ agencies supported and 125,000+ hours reclaimed. Both are undated marketing counts with no stated period. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **selection_conflict** — The selection funnel is described two different ways. The homepage claims only the top 6% of candidates advance through a 7-stage screen (background, English fluency, domain knowledge, security training, live simulation, SOP audit, manager interview). How We Work instead describes 300+ resumes reviewed monthly, 100 candidates hired for evaluation, only 50% advancing, and 50 certified EVAs. The two do not reconcile. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **testimonials** — Published testimonials are attributed to partial names — Mark L. (agency owner), Jennifer R. (operations manager) and Brian Casey (insurance agency owner) — and include a claim of 10 hours a week regained. None is dated or tied to a named agency. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- About Us — SecureEVAs — https://www.secureevas.com/about/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- How SecureEVAs Works — https://www.secureevas.com/how-it-works/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Insurance Support — SecureEVAs — https://www.secureevas.com/insurance/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- FAQ — SecureEVAs — https://www.secureevas.com/faq/ (seen 2026-09-09)

The service standard, and who owns the client relationship, remain the agency's decisions. |

Delegating to a remote seat cannot work until the process is written, which makes this the session where the decision is really made. |

That is a real onboarding standard to compare your own against. It trains the vendor's people, not yours, so it supports the session rather than delivering it. |

The relevance is that removing admin from a producer's week raises their sales focus percentage, and that an EVA seat is a live alternative to a hire you were about to make. Treat it as a staffing-math input, not a sales capability. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: List every service task done last week, mark each as must-be-licensed, must-be-local, or delegable, then total the delegable hours.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Service turnaround time and delegable admin hours removed from licensed staff per week.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name the one process that only works because a specific person is in the building, then decide who writes it down this month.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Percentage of delegable workflows with a written SOP an outsider could execute.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Write what a new team member must be able to do unsupervised by day one, then check what your current onboarding actually delivers.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Days from start to first unsupervised task, for a remote seat versus an in-house hire.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Total the admin hours currently sitting on your producers, convert to selling hours, and price that against the hire you were planning.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Producer selling hours per week before and after admin is offloaded.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Trained virtual assistants who take on recurring administrative and operational workload.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves work that only stays with you because nobody else has been handed it.
- **Growth Through Service Resources** (/resources/growth-through-service)
  - Helps with: Trained virtual assistants who take on service, administrative and back-office workload.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves moving routine work off licensed staff so capacity opens up.

**Open questions**

- Pricing entirely. No hourly rate, monthly rate, minimum hours or contract term is published; the only route is a discovery call. The '~60% lower cost than in-house' figure is a comparison, not a price.
- SOC 2 Type 2 and HIPAA claims are stated but no report, auditor, audit period or Trust Services scope is published. Ask for the report under NDA before relying on the claim.
- Where EVAs are located, whether they are employees or contractors, and under which jurisdiction's law policyholder data is processed.
- Whether EVAs are licensed for any state, and exactly which tasks must stay with licensed agency staff.
- Which AMS, rater and carrier systems EVAs actually work in, and how access is granted and revoked.
- The FAQ answers beyond the first question did not render on the page observed, so published detail on onboarding duration, task scope, data-security specifics, EVA departure handling and SOP prerequisites was not read.
- The selection funnel is described two irreconcilable ways across the homepage and How We Work; neither version was verifiable.
- Client retention is published as both 99% and 97% on the same site on the same day.
- Hours of coverage, time-zone overlap with a US agency, and holiday or absence cover.
- Whether the vendor carries E&O or cyber liability, and how a data incident would be handled and disclosed.
- Onboarding duration in concrete days, and what the agency must supply before an EVA can start.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_partner_organization_bound
- disclosure: Research compiled from SecureEVAs' own public website only. No representative was contacted, no audit report was seen, and no claim was independently verified.

### 4. Standard

- **Tier:** Platinum
- **Website:** https://standardplaybook.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/the-standard.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `standard`
- **Category:** Insurance agency coaching, producer sales training and agency operating software
- **Also known as:** The Standard Playbook, Standard Playbook Inc, Standard, Standard Call Scoring
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (23 researched facts)**

- **founder** — The Standard Playbook is founded and led by Justin Harkelroad, described as founder and head coach with 20 years in the insurance industry who built, scaled and sold three Allstate agencies. The company positions itself as built by operators rather than gurus. _(public_company_claim)_
- **location** — The company is based in Fort Wayne, Indiana and states that it serves insurance agencies nationwide. _(public_company_claim)_
- **offering_shape** — The offering is coaching, software and AI for insurance agency owners, organised around a Body, Being, Balance and Business frame. _(public_company_claim)_
- **directive** — The Directive is private 1:1 coaching, application only, aimed at owners who know the strategy but are not executing it. _(public_company_claim)_
- **directive_rhythm** — The Directive is described as one 2-hour private session each month, a weekly check-in protocol, a monthly mission check-in and one 2-hour group Boardroom call. _(public_company_claim)_
- **boardroom** — The Boardroom is the group mastermind format, and a Boardroom call is included in The Directive. _(public_company_claim)_
- **certified_standard** — Certified Standard is one-off AI sales training for producers at $399 per seat, one time, with no coaching program, subscription or application required. It trains the full Hello-to-Bind producer call. _(public_company_claim)_
- **certified_contents** — Certified Standard is stated to contain 24 training modules, 18 live AI voice roleplay scenarios and 85 AI quiz prompts across 17 scored quizzes, with an 80 percent default certification threshold. The site labels these product facts about course contents, not claims of results. _(public_company_claim)_
- **certified_evidence** — Certified Standard saves every practice call as a transcript and returns scores and completion data, so an owner can see who is certified. _(public_company_claim)_
- **eight_week** — The 8-Week Sales Management Experience is a fixed eight-week sprint in which a sales manager and team author the sales process, numbers and consequences the agency runs on, signed and operating by week eight. _(public_company_claim)_
- **eight_week_artifacts** — The signed system produced by the 8-Week Experience is described as a talk path, daily activity floor, monthly production line, follow-up standard and consequence ladder, authored by the team rather than handed to them. _(public_company_claim)_
- **eight_week_rhythm** — The 8-Week Experience runs on a stated rhythm: a weekday voice note to the manager in Telegram with a reply expected, one live weekly call that locks that week's document, a Monday training video for producers and a Wednesday workbook applied to real calls. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agency_brain** — Agency Brain is the platform the programs run on, described as pulling pipeline, team, training, retention and daily habits into one place. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ab_pipeline** — Agency Brain lists a sales dashboard, sales analytics broken down by date, source and bundle, pipeline intelligence by lead and stage, and marketing ROI by channel. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ab_retention** — Agency Brain lists AI call scoring with execution checklists and talk-to-listen ratios, renewal tracking, a cancel audit covering at-risk and saved premium, and a winback area. Call scoring is also sold standalone as Standard Call Scoring. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ab_team** — Agency Brain lists a team training library, an AI roleplay trainer, habit tracking built on Core 4 plus Flow with 56 points a week and team leaderboards, and target setting presented as a 90-day action map broken into daily habits. _(public_company_claim)_
- **flows** — Agency Brain ships seven structured daily Flows — War, Idea, Discovery, Irritation, Gratitude and Prayer among them — each a defined practice for a specific reality of the operator's day, and each ending in an action. _(public_company_claim)_
- **built_not_bought** — The company states that Justin writes the code himself and that every AI tool inside Agency Brain, including call scoring, the roleplay bot, the voice trainer and the discovery coach, is built in-house rather than bought or licensed. _(public_company_claim)_
- **training_library** — Team Training is an on-demand library covering sales, service, management, AI and Agency Brain itself, included in every Agency Brain account, with a stated ability to turn any video into a structured team training. _(public_company_claim)_
- **library_tracks** — The training library is organised as tracks with lanes for producers, service reps and managers. Named tracks include The Quoted Households Math, Cancellation Requests and The Three Buckets Method. _(public_company_claim)_
- **directive_availability** — The Directive page displayed SOLD OUT when observed. Program availability changes and was not confirmed with the company. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **six_week** — A 6 Week Producer Challenge is referenced as a daily-accountability add-on to Certified Standard. No further detail was published on the pages observed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **agency_count** — The claim of three Allstate agencies built, scaled and sold is the company's own public statement and was not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- The Standard Playbook — About — https://standardplaybook.com/about (seen 2026-09-09)
- The Directive — 1:1 Coaching — https://standardplaybook.com/directive (seen 2026-09-09)

The owner still decides who trains, when, and what happens when someone does not pass. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one call moment, name the standard for it, then decide the gate: what score, by when, and who reviews the transcript.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Producers certified against the gate, and practice transcripts reviewed per week.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Write the one call your whole team runs, then name the two checkpoints you would score it on.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Percentage of scored calls that follow the written sequence.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name the one process that currently only works because you are in the room, and decide who authors it.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the written standard exists, is signed, and is being run without the owner present.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Define the one service moment you would never let vary, then decide how you would know it happened.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: At-risk premium identified versus premium saved, week by week.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: List your channels and write what each one actually produced last month, not what you hoped.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Leads, quotes, premium and commission attributed per channel.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Growth strategy, sales systems, producer standards and management rhythm across the opportunity stack.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves diagnosing where the growth system is breaking and building the operating standard around it.
- **Formula Sales Sequence Resources** (/resources/sales-sequence)
  - Helps with: Producer systems, sales management, accountability, coaching and call performance.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves producer consistency, sales standards, management rhythm or coaching.
- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Operating standards, role ownership, management rhythm and documented ways of working.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves removing yourself from the center of decisions the team should be making.
- **Training Resources** (/resources/training)
  - Helps with: Onboarding structure, role standards, coaching rhythm and repeatable team development.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves how people are trained, what standard they are trained to, and who holds it.
- **Body Resources** (/resources/body)
  - Helps with: Building repeatable standards across Body, Being, Balance and Business.
  - Best fit: Your Body Domino requires more structure, accountability or consistency rather than another burst of motivation.
- **Balance Resources** (/resources/balance)
  - Helps with: Building intentional standards across the Core 4 rather than allowing Business to consume everything else.
  - Best fit: Your Balance Domino requires a repeatable rhythm that makes the people you value visible in your actual calendar and behavior.
- **Being Resources** (/resources/being)
  - Helps with: Applying the Core 4 framework to the standards a leader chooses to live across business and life.
  - Best fit: Your Being Domino requires a repeatable practice that brings you back to truth, alignment and intentional action.

**Open questions**

- The exact Formula partnerOrgId, the authenticated representative UID and verified membership. An active organization named Standard Playbook Inc exists with website standardplaybook.com, but no representative account was verified.
- Pricing for The Boardroom, The Directive, the Partnership tier and Agency Brain plans. Only the $399 Certified Standard seat price is published on the pages observed; every other price is quoted privately and is deliberately absent here.
- Current program availability. The Directive showed SOLD OUT when observed and availability was not confirmed with the company.
- Any outcome, retention, close-rate or production figures. The site publishes product contents rather than results, and none were asserted.
- Standard Playbook's own internal org chart, assigned owners, capacity, budget, baseline KPIs and 2027 targets.
- The relationship between Agency Brain plan tiers and which programs include which features.
- Whether the 6 Week Producer Challenge is currently sold, and on what terms.

### 5. EverQuote

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://www.everquote.com/pro/
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/everquote.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `everquote`
- **Category:** Online insurance lead marketplace selling data leads, inbound and warm-transfer calls, managed paid search and outsourced lead outreach to agents
- **Also known as:** EverQuote, EverQuote Pro, EverQuote for Agents, EVER
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (26 researched facts)**

- **what** — EverQuote for Agents sells insurance agents access to an online insurance marketplace, positioning itself as an all-in-one growth partner rather than only a lead vendor. EverQuote is listed on NASDAQ under EVER. The agent site resolves to go.everquote.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **product_lines** — Four product areas are published: Marketing Services (managed paid search), Leads and Calls, Lead Connection Service (outsourced lead outreach), and Agent Support delivered through the Accelerated Growth Program. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lead_products** — The buyable products are auto data leads, home data leads, auto consumer-initiated inbound calls and home consumer-initiated inbound calls. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lead_tiers** — Each product line breaks into lead types such as Premium, Preferred, Standard and Non-Standard, which EverQuote states are aligned to common carrier underwriting requirements. Most agents get a choice of 4 auto lead types, 4 auto warm-transfer call types, 2 home lead types and 2 home call types. Available types vary by the agent's appointments and carrier relationships. _(public_company_claim)_
- **targeting** — Agents control risk profile filters, ZIP-code-level territory, the time of day and days of week they receive leads, and a daily lead cap. EverQuote states a lead is only sent if it matches the agent's risk filters, sits in a targeted ZIP code and arrives during the agent's designated hours. _(public_company_claim)_
- **enhanced_targeting** — Enhanced Targeting lets an agent bid up on specific lead attributes, with high-intent shoppers, small business owners and driver age named as examples. _(public_company_claim)_
- **shared_and_exclusive** — Shared leads go to a maximum of 3 agents and only one agent per carrier, so an agent never competes with more than 2 other agents or with another agent from their own carrier. Exclusive auto leads are available at an additional cost per lead and go to one agent only, but an account must also elect shared leads to receive them, and exclusives are delivered only as available. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sourcing** — Leads come from two sources: EverQuote's owned and operated online properties, and its Verified Partner Network of third-party insurance shopping websites. EverQuote states Verified Partner Network leads are sold exclusively through EverQuote and to a maximum of three agents. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pro_platform** — EverQuote Pro is the lead management platform, included at no extra cost with every EverQuote lead or call. It is self-service: territories, lead types, lead cap, delivery settings and payment options are all changeable by the agent after signup, with real-time email notification for every lead or call. _(public_company_claim)_
- **user_roles** — An agency can add unlimited users to EverQuote Pro through Account then User Management. There are three roles: Customer Admin controls all aspects of the account, Limited Customer Admin can pause the account and return leads, and User can view but not change anything. _(public_company_claim)_
- **delivery** — Data leads are delivered by email or directly into the agent's lead management system, with the agent choosing. EverQuote Pro integrates with a range of lead management systems and raters, with the current list available from an account manager rather than published. _(public_company_claim)_
- **calls** — Calls come from inbound and outbound sources. For inbound, a shopper calls EverQuote's number, EverQuote collects basic information and consent, then transfers the live call to the agent's phone. For outbound warm transfers, EverQuote operators dial and qualify the prospect before transferring. Home warm transfers are currently outbound only. On either path the agent has 120 seconds to keep or reject the lead at no cost. _(public_company_claim)_
- **billing** — Billing is pay-as-you-go with an automatic credit card recharge the agent configures. For data-lead-only accounts the card is charged when the balance falls to $25 or below, with a warning email at $50 or below. For accounts buying consumer-initiated calls the recharge triggers at $100 or below with a warning email at $200 or below. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_contract** — EverQuote states there is no minimum period and no minimum spend, and that an account can be cancelled immediately with no questions asked. The unused account balance is fully refunded except where the remaining balance is a non-refundable initial deposit match credit. _(public_company_claim)_
- **returns** — Return credit requests are submitted in EverQuote Pro up to 30 days after a lead's purchase date, capped at 20 percent of the total leads purchased by the account in a calendar month. The process is fully automated with no call or email needed up to that threshold, and anything beyond 20 percent is rejected at month end for any reason. Returns may sit in Processing until enough additional leads are purchased to clear the monthly calculation. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pause** — The account can be paused at any time by a Customer Admin or Limited Customer Admin, including pausing with a scheduled resume time, and Customer Admins can set specific hours of operation so leads only arrive when the team can work them. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lead_connection** — Lead Connection Service is EverQuote's outsourced outreach: its specialists call the agent's leads on a data-driven cadence, contacting a lead within 30 seconds and following up for up to seven days by phone and by email that highlights the agency, then passing qualified prospects to the agent. _(public_company_claim)_
- **marketing_services** — Marketing Services is managed paid search run by EverQuote's digital marketing team to increase an agency's online visibility. The product overview page states availability is limited by carrier. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agp** — The Accelerated Growth Program is aimed at large agencies seeking aggressive growth. Published benefits are pricing based on the agency's ROI and growth goals, priority access to available lead volume, a dedicated Agency Business Consultant with specialised training and monthly business reviews, and first access to new offerings and beta releases. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agp_partner_network** — AGP members get access to a curated partner discount marketplace spanning hiring and recruiting (IdealTraits, ALLRecruiting), coaching and team training (RPOAi, Insurance Teamwork Services), business brokerage (AgencyForSale), banking (Wintrust), accounting and back office (Club Capital), and agency consulting and technology implementation (Agency Zoomify for AgencyZoom). _(public_company_claim)_
- **agp_partner_terms** — Published AGP partner offers include $300 off any IdealTraits standard package with complimentary video interviews and workflow automation, 50 percent off the first month of RPOAi, a waived $750 Club Capital onboarding fee, 10 percent off the Agency Zoomify Pro Package, a one-time $350 Wintrust credit toward a sponsored Allstate conference, and free consultation or valuation calls from ALLRecruiting, Insurance Teamwork Services and AgencyForSale. _(public_company_claim)_
- **referral** — Current customers can earn up to $1,000 for each referred agent who becomes a customer: a $250 EverQuote Pro credit if the referred agent signs up for leads, or a $1,000 credit or gift card if they enrol in AGP. Referrals are submitted through a PartnerStack portal and bonuses take up to 10 business days to process. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pricing_not_published** — EverQuote states lead prices vary by product type, lead type or risk profile, and the agent's eligibility for subsidy programs, and directs pricing questions to sales rather than publishing a rate. AGP pricing is described as goal-based. No lead price appears anywhere on the public site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **onboarding_contact** — Signup runs through a form followed by a representative who walks the agency through setup and provides an estimate of lead volume available in that territory. Contacts are sales@everquote.com, support@everquote.com and (844) 707-8800, weekdays 9AM to 5PM Eastern. EverQuote states it works with both captive and independent agents. _(public_company_claim)_
- **scale_claims** — Undated marketing figures on the agent home page include 2M+ monthly online shoppers, 10K agents, 15+ years of industry expertise, and the claims of being the number one online insurance lead marketplace and America's largest online insurance marketplace. None were independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **outcome_claims** — EverQuote publishes outcome figures for Lead Connection Service of 3x more leads and a 30 percent higher bind rate, alongside agent testimonials claiming doubled or tripled policy counts, a 3X increase in new business premium, $1.9 million in sales in a year with 95 percent attributed to EverQuote, and $100,000 in premium written per $10,000 spent. These are vendor-published results and testimonials, not verified outcomes, and must not be repeated as fact. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- EverQuote for Agents — Home — https://go.everquote.com/pro (seen 2026-09-09)
- EverQuote Product Overview — https://go.everquote.com/products (seen 2026-09-09)
- EverQuote for Agents — Frequently Asked Questions — https://go.everquote.com/pro/faq (seen 2026-09-09)
- Exclusive AGP Partner Discounts for Insurance Agencies — https://go.everquote.com/partners (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Making It Rain | primary | The Opportunity Distribution | Leads, calls and managed paid search are three separate purchased channels with real controls — ZIP codes, risk tiers, hours, daily cap, attribute-level bid-ups, exclusive versus shared. That makes the opportunity distribution explicit and comparable. It is spend, not strategy: the owner still decides how much of the pipeline should be bought and at what cost per policy. |
| The Sales Sequence | primary | Accountability | Paid leads make a sales sequence measurable in a way organic leads do not: every lead has a cost, a timestamp and a return window, and a warm transfer gives the producer 120 seconds to decide. Lead Connection Service also forces a real decision about which part of the sequence the agency owns and which part it outsources. That decision, and the consequence when the standard is missed, is the owner's. |
| The Operating System | supporting_direct | Your Agency Bible | Three named permission levels, an auto-recharge threshold, a 20 percent monthly return ceiling and a 30-day return window are operating rules with money attached. Who holds Customer Admin, who may pause the account and who submits returns is exactly the kind of thing that should be written rather than assumed. |
| Commitment to Training | conditional | The GOATs Train Every Day | The AGP partner marketplace routes an agency to third-party recruiting, coaching and implementation vendors, and the AGP business consultant runs monthly reviews. Conditional because none of that is EverQuote's own curriculum, there is no scoring or completion evidence, and every partner offer is a separate purchase to evaluate on its own terms. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Elite Onboarding Experience | Conditional. A purchased shopper is not yet a served client, and up to three agents may be working the same person. Lead Connection Service touches the prospect in the agency's name before the agency does, which is a brand and service question worth deciding deliberately rather than by default. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Write what each channel produced last month in leads, quotes and bound premium, then set the number a purchased lead has to beat.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Cost per bound policy by channel, and the share of new business coming from purchased opportunity.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Set the speed-to-contact standard on a paid lead, then decide out loud whether your team makes those first calls or someone else does.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Minutes to first contact, contact rate, and quote rate per purchased lead by producer.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Assign the three EverQuote Pro roles by name, then write down who watches the balance and who submits returns each week.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Return rate against the 20 percent ceiling, and whether any lead aged past the 30-day return window unclaimed.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Role play the opening 20 seconds of a call to a shopper who has already spoken to two other agents today.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Contact-to-quote rate on purchased leads by producer, before and after the training changes.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Decide what a prospect should hear in the first outreach made in your agency's name, and whether you are comfortable outsourcing it.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Retention at first renewal on purchased-lead business compared with referral business.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Insurance leads, calls and shopper traffic sold directly to agents.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves adding a steady source of quotable opportunity.

**Open questions**

- Lead and call prices. EverQuote states they vary by product, lead type and subsidy eligibility and publishes none. AGP pricing is described only as goal-based.
- Which carrier subsidy programs an agency qualifies for and what the subsidy is worth.
- The cost premium on exclusive auto leads, and how often exclusives are actually available.
- Whether life leads are still sold to agents. Life appears in the pricing FAQ's list of product types but not in the published product lineup of auto and home leads and calls.
- The current list of lead management systems and raters EverQuote Pro integrates with. It is available only from an account manager.
- The threshold to qualify for the Accelerated Growth Program, and the size of agency it is aimed at beyond the word large.
- The size of the non-refundable initial deposit match credit, which is the one carve-out from the full refund on cancellation.
- Actual close rates, cost per policy or return on ad spend for any agency. The published 3x and 30 percent figures and the testimonial numbers are vendor claims and were not verified.
- Whether Marketing Services is available to a given agency, since the site states availability is limited by carrier without naming which.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_representative_checked
- disclosure: Research compiled from EverQuote's public agent-facing website only, including its published agent FAQ. No account, contract, rate card or platform login was seen, and no EverQuote representative reviewed this record. The requested URL everquote.com/pro redirects to go.everquote.com/pro, which is the page actually observed.

### 6. Filtered Quotes

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://filteredquotes.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/filtered-quotes.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `filtered-quotes`
- **Category:** Insurance lead generation, acquisition analytics and remote sales performance management
- **Also known as:** Filtered Quotes, LLC, Buy Filtered, buyfiltered.com, Filtered
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Jared Phillips
- Vimeo id: 1222214833
- Contact: Sign up → https://filteredquotes.com
- Shown as: filteredquotes.com
- Note: Jared shared Filtered Quotes as the place to sign up and get taken care of.

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **entity** — The company is Filtered Quotes, LLC. The buyfiltered.com domain redirects to filteredquotes.com, and both domains are used: the site is branded Filtered Quotes while its published email addresses are sales@buyfiltered.com and support@buyfiltered.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact** — Published contact details are phone (678) 921-9958, sales@buyfiltered.com and support@buyfiltered.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **four_offers** — The offering is presented as four service areas: lead generation, Remote Performance Management, spam remediation, and a long-term partnership programme called ACES. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lead_lines** — Lead programmes cover health, home and auto insurance. Home leads are positioned both as standalone production and as part of a cross-sell and bundling strategy for multi-line agencies. _(public_company_claim)_
- **delivery** — Lead delivery options published are exclusive and shared, direct post and ping post, in real time, with transparent reporting. _(public_company_claim)_
- **origin_thesis** — The stated founding thesis is that the industry asked the wrong question. Instead of how many leads did I buy, Filtered Quotes says the better question is how much revenue those leads actually created. _(public_company_claim)_
- **evolution** — The company states it has evolved beyond lead generation and now combines exclusive lead generation, performance analytics, sales process optimisation, call accountability and remote performance management into one growth system. _(public_company_claim)_
- **tech_areas** — Stated technology investment areas are advanced filtering, fraud detection, spam remediation, deliverability monitoring, source optimisation, and AI-driven pricing intended to control acquisition cost. _(public_company_claim)_
- **filter_fields** — Depending on campaign and source, leads may carry validation and filtering on phone, email, geographic location, consumer demographics, product-specific criteria, IP address and device information, consent documentation, TrustedForm or Jornaya certification, and source and campaign information. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_guarantee** — The leads page states plainly that no validation process can guarantee that every consumer will answer, qualify or purchase, and frames the goal as a more transparent acquisition process evaluated on real data. _(public_company_claim)_
- **compliance** — The company states its lead programmes are built around documented consumer inquiries, and that available data may include consent language, landing-page information, certification records, IP address and user agent depending on source. _(public_company_claim)_
- **two_programs** — There are two published programme tiers. The Standard programme has no long-term commitment and no required monthly minimum, is self-directed, and requires the agency to create an account and manage its own funding. _(public_company_claim)_
- **aces_threshold** — The ACES Partnership is stated to be designed for agencies investing at least $2,500 per month, and includes a dedicated Client Success Manager, proactive account strategy, ongoing account review and optimisation, and faster personalised support. _(public_company_claim)_
- **aces_values** — ACES is also the company's stated operating standard: Radical Accountability, Sincere Candor, Intentional Execution and Unreasonable Standards. _(public_company_claim)_
- **rpm_scope** — Remote Performance Management is described on the home page as sales coaching, call reviews, producer accountability, performance metrics, role playing and sales leadership. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pages_under_construction** — The dedicated Remote Performance Management, Spam Remediation and How We Can Help pages were all marked under construction when observed, so RPM and spam remediation are described only at home-page depth. _(public_company_claim)_
- **spam_scope** — Spam remediation is described as continuous monitoring of the agency's phone numbers, carrier and third-party spam remediation, caller reputation management and ongoing protection, with the goal of restoring answer rates. _(public_company_claim)_
- **calculator** — The site publishes a working Acquisition Economics calculator that derives break-even and target cost per item or acquisition from average annual premium, new business commission, renewal commission, first-year retention rate and desired profit margin. _(public_company_claim)_
- **calc_modes** — The calculator forces a choice between being profitable at new business or at first renewal, computes revenue through the first renewal, and then compares a real marketing spend and items-written figure against that break-even. _(public_company_claim)_
- **resources_pending** — Two further resources, an Agency Performance Diagnostic and a Lead Follow-Up Guide, are published as Coming Soon rather than as available. _(public_company_claim)_
- **team** — The published team names Jared Phillips as Founder, Head of Revenue and Megan Balfe as Founder, Head of Operations, plus a Director of RPM, two Client Success Managers, a Head of Finance and three sales roles. _(public_company_claim)_
- **segments** — The leads page states the company supports agencies at different stages, from individual agents and small teams through organisations managing significant volume across multiple producers and locations. _(public_company_claim)_
- **portal** — Lead delivery and account management run through a separate Lead Portal at filteredquotes.leadportal.com, and a separate Courses login exists for training content. _(public_company_claim)_
- **marketing_counts** — The How We Can Help page displays 15+ Years of Experience and 2M+ Leads Delivered. Both are undated marketing counts with no methodology published. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **testimonial_outcomes** — Published testimonials from Allstate agency owners and an office manager claim consistent conversations, real opportunities and one of the best ROIs they have seen. These are unverified customer outcome claims. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Filtered Quotes — Home — https://filteredquotes.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Filtered Quotes — About Us — https://filteredquotes.com/about-us (seen 2026-09-09)
- Filtered Quotes — Leads — https://filteredquotes.com/leads (seen 2026-09-09)

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Name the one call moment you would want an outside reviewer to score, and decide who inside your agency owns the fix.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Producer calls reviewed per week, and how many produced a written correction.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Check whether your outbound service numbers are being labelled as spam, and decide who owns that check monthly.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Answer rate on outbound service calls, tracked before and after any remediation.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Write the acquisition rule your agency runs on: what you will pay, by channel, before you cut it.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the spend decision is made against a written break-even rather than in the moment.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Decide whether your follow-up standard is written down anywhere, and if not, who writes it this week.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Whether a written follow-up standard exists and is being practised against real calls.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Run your real average premium, commissions and first-year retention through the break-even maths, then write the highest CPA you can defend.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Actual cost per item written per channel, measured against your own break-even CPI.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Insurance lead generation alongside sales performance support and phone-number spam remediation.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves buying opportunity and having a partner help you work it.

**Open questions**

- All pricing. No cost per lead, per programme fee or RPM price is published. The only figure on the site is the $2,500 per month minimum investment that qualifies an agency for ACES, which is a threshold rather than a price.
- Remote Performance Management delivery detail: cadence, who delivers it, whether it is included in ACES or sold separately, and what a call review actually produces. The product page was under construction when observed.
- Spam remediation delivery detail, vendor relationships and pricing. That product page was also under construction.
- Lead sourcing. The site describes filtering and compliance practices but does not name its lead sources, publishers or how exclusivity is enforced.
- Return, credit or replacement policy on bad leads. No policy is published.
- Which states, carriers or products the health, home and auto programmes actually cover.
- The relationship between the buyfiltered.com and filteredquotes.com domains. buyfiltered.com redirects to filteredquotes.com, but the company's published email addresses remain on buyfiltered.com; bind the partner organization to whichever hostname its record already carries.
- Company founding date, headquarters and size. The team page names nine people but publishes no location, founding year or headcount.
- The Courses product behind the separate Courses login: what it contains, who it is for and whether it is sold.
- Any outcome data. The 15+ years and 2M+ leads figures are undated marketing counts, and the ROI language on the site comes from customer testimonials.
