### 7. Hagerty

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://www.hagerty.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/hagerty.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `hagerty`
- **Category:** Specialty collector and classic vehicle insurance, distributed through agents and direct, plus marketplace, valuation and media brands
- **Also known as:** Hagerty Insurance, Hagerty Insurance Agency, LLC, The Hagerty Group, LLC, Hagerty Agent Business Center
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (22 researched facts)**

- **positioning** — Hagerty sells insurance for drivers of classic and enthusiast vehicles, and runs adjacent brands around that audience: Hagerty Drivers Club, Hagerty Marketplace auctions and classifieds, valuation tools, media, Broad Arrow auctions, and events including The Amelia, Greenwich Concours, California Mille, Motorlux and RADwood. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_channel** — Hagerty runs a separate agent-facing site, the Hagerty Agent Business Center at hagertyagent.com, with agency sign-up, agent login, a products area and eligibility guidelines. Agent support is 800-747-5348 and agent@hagerty.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_signup** — An agency signs up through an online form and Hagerty states approval takes 1 business day, with a representative contacting the agency within 1 business day. Hagerty states agencies can start quoting while approval is in progress. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_production_requirements** — Hagerty states there are no production requirements for appointed agents. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_admin** — The agent toolkit includes downloading policies into the agency management system, direct client billing, policy documents, and direct deposit of commissions. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_marketing_support** — Hagerty publishes marketing materials for agents, tips for generating leads at local car events, online training, and vehicle valuation tools. _(public_company_claim)_
- **guaranteed_value** — The core product feature is Guaranteed Value: Hagerty and the client agree on a value up front and a total loss pays that value, less any deductible and any salvage value retained. Guaranteed Value is a registered trademark of The Hagerty Group. _(public_company_claim)_
- **usage_rule** — Usage is the defining eligibility rule: the vehicle may be used for club functions, exhibitions, organized meets, tours and occasional pleasure driving, but it cannot be the client's daily driver. Regular driving to work or errands for up to 5 months a year may be considered under a Seasonal Driver arrangement. _(public_company_claim)_
- **mileage** — Hagerty states mileage of 3,500 or less is generally consistent with a collectible-value vehicle, and mileage up to 7,500 may be considered. Usage and mileage requirements differ by vehicle type, such as collector trucks or vehicles 1990 and newer. _(public_company_claim)_
- **storage** — Enclosed secure storage is preferred, but carports, parking garages, car hauling trailers and driveways may be considered with published limits: carports outside CA and parts of FL up to $100k vehicle value and more than 5 miles from the coast in FL and LA; driveways outside CA, CO, FL and HI up to $50,000 value and not within 50 miles of the LA coast. Tents, domes, streets, parking lots and public parking garages are unacceptable. _(public_company_claim)_
- **driver_eligibility** — In most states Hagerty cannot cover a driver with a serious infraction in the last three years, defined as alcohol-related offenses, reckless driving or excessive speed violations. All household driving records are underwriter reviewed and one or two minor violations or accidents are generally acceptable. Drivers as young as 16 with a clean record may drive while supervised. _(public_company_claim)_
- **daily_use_requirement** — Every licensed household member must have a regular-use vehicle for daily driving and must maintain regular-use insurance in their own name. Motorcycles and public transportation are not considered viable daily-use vehicles in most cases. Clients with fewer than one daily-use vehicle per driver may still be considered. _(public_company_claim)_
- **limits** — Hagerty encourages liability limits less than or equal to the limits on the client's Personal Auto Policy. Business Auto Policies are considered when a Drive Other Car or Individual Named Insured endorsement is attached, except in GA, KS, MN, NY and NC where only state minimum limits are offered to an applicant carrying only a Business Auto Policy. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ineligible** — Vehicles that generally do not qualify include off-road and recreational vehicles, daily-use vehicles, commercial-use vehicles and motorcycles with performance modifications. Hagerty states it does not have a price for every risk and decides eligibility on the accumulation of all risk characteristics against program guidelines. _(public_company_claim)_
- **vehicle_classes** — Published vehicle appetite covers collector and classic cars 1979 and older, collector trucks and SUVs 1998 and older, 1980-and-newer limited editions, exotics and supercars, modified rods and replicas, motorsports vehicles, military vehicles 20 years and older, retired commercial vehicles 25 years and older, classic and antique tractors 25 years and older, collector and raced motorcycles and scooters, boats, and vintage camper trailers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **additional_coverages** — Optional coverages listed are Cherished Salvage (keep a totaled car and still receive full Guaranteed Value), Evacuation Expense (reimburses 50% of the cost of moving a covered vehicle for emergencies such as hurricane or wildfire), Vehicle Under Construction (recognises rising value during restoration), spare parts, automotive tools and automobilia on the same policy, and Motorsports Advantage for on-track activity. _(public_company_claim)_
- **business_lines** — Hagerty also writes commercial business for the collector-car trade: restoration shop, museum, dealership, storage facility and collector car club insurance. Commercial contact is commercial@hagerty.com and 888-216-2420. _(public_company_claim)_
- **underwriters** — Personal collector policies are underwritten by Essentia Insurance Company, and the business insurance products are underwritten by Markel Insurance Company, Markel American Insurance Company or Travelers. Insurance services are administered by Hagerty Insurance Agency, LLC, and Hagerty determines final risk acceptance. _(public_company_claim)_
- **state_variation** — Hagerty repeats across product pages that not all coverages are available in every state and that storage and driver eligibility are subject to underwriter review and applicable state restrictions, so appetite must be checked per state before quoting. _(public_company_claim)_
- **client_value_props** — Client-facing differentiators listed for agents are Guaranteed Value, claims staff and parts specialists trained in classic vehicle repair, collector-car experts available seven days a week, flexible pleasure and hobby usage, youthful operator allowance, and coverage breadth across cars, trucks, motorcycles, boats, modified cars and sports cars. _(public_company_claim)_
- **community_claim** — Hagerty describes the Hagerty Drivers Club as the world's largest community of driving enthusiasts. This is the company's own undated marketing claim and was not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **agent_login_change** — A notice on the agent site stated that beginning in March, agents may be asked to verify identity with multi-factor authentication when accessing the Agent Business Center, and advised agencies sharing a login to alert their admin. The year was not stated on the page and the current status was not confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Hagerty for Agents — Become a Hagerty Classic Car Insurance Agent — https://www.hagertyagent.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Eligibility Guidelines — Hagerty Insurance Agent Business Center — https://www.hagertyagent.com/insurance/eligibility-guidelines (seen 2026-09-09)

- About Hagerty — Corporate Profile — https://www.hagerty.com/about-us (seen 2026-09-09)

Hagerty publishes the marketing materials, the local-event lead tips and the valuation tools that make that lane workable, and its appetite list tells an owner exactly which households in the book contain one. The strategy, the spend and the follow-through still belong to the agency. |

Someone in the agency still has to author that page and own it. |

The service standard itself, and who owns it, stays with the agency. |

Conditional because the material is carrier-product knowledge with no scoring, no gate and no evidence of who completed it; the assessment layer has to come from the agency. |

| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: List the vehicles in your current book that would qualify under a collector program, then decide who owns the outreach and by when.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Collector-vehicle quotes generated per month and the source that produced each one.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Write the four qualifying questions a producer must ask before quoting a collector vehicle, in the order they get asked.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Percentage of collector submissions that clear underwriting on the first pass.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name the one carrier or program whose submission process currently lives only in someone's head, and decide who writes it down this week.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the written submission standard exists and is being followed without the owner in the room.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Define the one review moment where a collector vehicle gets surfaced on an existing household, and decide how you would know it happened.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Existing households reviewed for collector exposure per month.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one collector-appetite rule and decide how a producer would prove they know it before quoting unsupervised.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Producers who can state the usage and daily-use rules without looking them up.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Collector-vehicle submission, billing and commission mechanics you can turn into a written standard.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves a carrier process that only one person on the team knows how to run.

**Open questions**

- Commission rates, contract terms and any appointment requirements. None are published on the public agent site and none were assumed here.
- Premium pricing for any collector product. Quoting is behind a login or a quote flow and no plain public price exists.
- Which states the agent program is currently open in, and whether appetite differs by state beyond the exclusions published in the eligibility guidelines.
- Whether the multi-factor authentication change flagged on the agent site is already live, and in which year the notice was posted.
- Any loss ratio, retention, close rate or production figure. Hagerty publishes product features rather than agent results and none were asserted.
- Whether Hagerty offers agency-level training, reporting or a book-transfer path beyond the online training and marketing materials named on the agent site.
- The relationship between the Hagerty Drivers Club membership and an agent-written policy.

### 8. QuoteWizard by LendingTree

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://agents.quotewizard.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/quotewizard.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `quotewizard`
- **Category:** Insurance lead generation: internet leads, clicks and live transfer calls sold to agents
- **Also known as:** QuoteWizard, QuoteWizard.com, WizardCalls, LendingTree Insurance
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **identity** — QuoteWizard is an insurance lead generation company founded in 2006 and is now part of the LendingTree network, publishing under the name QuoteWizard by LendingTree. Site legal footers identify LendingTree, LLC as the marketing lead generator of record. _(public_company_claim)_
- **what_is_sold** — What an agent buys is two product families: real-time internet web leads and live transfer calls. Published lead lines are auto, home, renters, health, life and Medicare, sold in all 50 states. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sourcing** — Leads originate from consumers who reach QuoteWizard.com and partner sites through search engine marketing and complete a detailed quote request form. The company states leads come from search or digital marketing on credible websites rather than incentivised or co-registration traffic. _(public_company_claim)_
- **delivery** — Leads are posted to the agent's online administration tool and also emailed. The company states it is integrated with many raters and lead management systems so leads can be delivered into the agent's own workflow. _(public_company_claim)_
- **matching** — After submission a lead is run through a validation process, then matched in real time to an agent based on the filters and territory that agent selected. The agent, not QuoteWizard, then contacts the shopper. _(public_company_claim)_
- **filters** — Filters are the agent's main quality control. Published auto examples include Multi-car, Owns home and No accidents; home filtering is described as dynamic, with examples such as excluding mobile homes and restricting geography. Filter selection is done with a specialised account representative. _(public_company_claim)_
- **distribution_cap** — QuoteWizard states it sells a web lead to a maximum of four agents. This is a published distribution ceiling, not an exclusivity guarantee. _(public_company_claim)_
- **volume_caps** — Agents set customizable daily and weekly caps to control how much lead volume reaches the team, alongside territory selection. _(public_company_claim)_
- **wizardcalls** — WizardCalls is the live transfer product. A US-based call center either receives calls from active policy shoppers or contacts them directly, verifies consumer intent against the agent's filter set, then connects the consumer to the agent for an introduction. If the agent misses the call, QuoteWizard states the agent is notified and still receives the consumer's information. _(public_company_claim)_
- **call_controls** — Live transfer controls the agent sets are a custom filter package, a territory, a daily availability schedule, and daily call and concurrency caps. Call recordings and customer details are available inside the QuoteWizard Agent Admin system. _(public_company_claim)_
- **billing** — Billing is pay-as-you-go against a prepaid balance. The credit card on file is charged an amount the agent sets at sign-up, and is re-charged automatically when the account balance falls below the cost of one lead. _(public_company_claim)_
- **pricing_model** — Lead price is described as the price of the lead plus the cost of any filters applied, so tighter filtering raises unit cost. No price is published; pricing is personalised and quoted by a sales representative, and monthly specials for new customers are quoted the same way. _(public_company_claim)_
- **returns** — Leads meeting a reason listed in QuoteWizard's Lead Credit Reason policy can be submitted online for account credit. The credit reason list itself was not published on the pages observed. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support** — The account comes with 24/7 self-service online account management plus a dedicated account representative, a customer support email address and a support phone line. _(public_company_claim)_
- **crm_integrations** — QuoteWizard publishes a CRM integration list naming Agency MVP, AgencyZoom, Blitz, Convoso, DYL, PhoneBurner, Ringy, Shape, VanillaSoft and Velocify among others, and names APEX LeadCloud as the official CRM of Farmers and Allstate Lead Manager as the official CRM of Allstate. Several partners are listed as carrying special offers for QuoteWizard clients. _(public_company_claim)_
- **rater_integrations** — Lead delivery also integrates with comparative raters; QuoteWizard names Applied, EZLynx, TurboRater and Vertafore PL Rating as supported rater systems and invites agents to ask about systems not on the list. _(public_company_claim)_
- **carrier_position** — QuoteWizard states it is an approved vendor with major insurance carriers, offers carrier-specific lead products, and that its live transfer product is eligible for select carrier subsidy programs. _(public_company_claim)_
- **education_program** — QuoteWizard runs an Agent Education Program: webinars, downloadable PDFs and agent case studies organised into Leads, Recruiting, Sales, Shorts, Business and Marketing tracks, plus a free Udemy certificate program on working and closing web and call leads. Much of the catalogue is contributed by third-party vendors and coaches rather than QuoteWizard itself. _(public_company_claim)_
- **elite_program** — An Elite Program is published for agents opening high-volume accounts; its terms are not stated on the public pages and require contacting sales. _(public_company_claim)_
- **footprint** — The company lists offices in Seattle, Denver and Beachwood (Cleveland), Ohio, and states it serves all 50 states. _(public_company_claim)_
- **scale_counts_unverified** — Marketing counts published without a stated measurement date: 20 years in business, 300+ employees, over 126,000 to 130,000 unique agents serviced, and over 50 million consumers who have used QuoteWizard. Not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **volume_claims_unverified** — Homepage marketing figures: 27K+ average web leads per day, 50% average quote rate on web leads, 30% reported close rate on live transfer leads, and over $100M spent each year on search engine marketing. These are vendor-published performance and volume claims, not confirmed results. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **close_rate_claim_unverified** — QuoteWizard states its search leads have proven to close at five times the rate of standard industry leads, footnoted to data generated by QuoteWizard, its network or affiliates in 2024. This is a vendor outcome claim and was not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **average_distribution_unverified** — Alongside the four-agent maximum, QuoteWizard states that due to territory restrictions and delivery schedules its leads are sold to under two agents on average. The average is a vendor-reported figure, unlike the four-agent cap which is stated as policy. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **bbb_claim_unverified** — QuoteWizard states it holds an A+ rating with the Better Business Bureau and that as part of LendingTree it is a publicly traded company. Not verified against BBB or filings. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Insurance Leads | Quality P&C, Health, & Life | QuoteWizard — https://agents.quotewizard.com/insurance-leads/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Auto Insurance Leads | High Quality Prospects | QuoteWizard — https://agents.quotewizard.com/auto-insurance-leads/ (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Making It Rain | primary | The Opportunity Distribution | QuoteWizard is a paid opportunity source: purchased web leads and live transfers, filtered and capped by the agency. That is squarely the distribution question this session asks an owner to decide. It does not decide the marketing strategy the spend sits inside. |
| The Sales Sequence | supporting_direct | Accountability | Delivery into a CRM, daily and concurrency caps, an availability schedule and call recordings give an owner the raw material to hold a producer seat accountable for what happens after a lead lands. The sequence itself, and who runs it, is the agency's to write. |
| The Operating System | conditional | Your Agency Bible | Caps, filters, prepaid billing and a credit-return process are operating decisions that belong written down rather than held in the owner's head. Conditional because QuoteWizard supplies the levers and the account, not the operating standard that governs them. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Elite Onboarding Experience | CRM and rater integrations decide how cleanly a bought opportunity lands in the systems your service team already uses, which is the handoff end of onboarding. Conditional because everything after the record arrives is the agency's own service standard. |
| Commitment to Training | conditional | The GOATs Train Every Day | The Agent Education Program is a real published library with webinars, PDFs and a free certificate course on working internet leads, so it can supply content for a training rhythm. Conditional because much of it is vendor-contributed marketing content and there is no scoring, gate or per-seat completion evidence. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Write your current opportunity mix by channel, then decide what percentage of next quarter's new opportunities you intend to buy versus generate.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Cost per lead, cost per quote and cost per bound policy for purchased leads, tracked separately from organic sources.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Name the speed-to-contact standard for a purchased lead and the number of attempts before it is dead, then decide who reviews the recordings.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Minutes to first contact per purchased lead and percentage of live transfers answered inside the availability window.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Write the one page that says who may change a cap or filter, what triggers a lead credit request, and what the account balance floor is.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the lead-buying rules are written, signed and being followed without the owner in the room.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Trace one purchased lead from delivery to bound policy and mark every place the record is re-keyed by hand.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Number of manual re-entry steps between lead delivery and a quote in the rater.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one lead-handling module, assign it with a due date, and decide how you will confirm it was actually watched.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Producers who completed the assigned module by the due date.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Web leads and live transfers sourced from insurance comparison traffic.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves opportunity you can turn on, filter and tune by volume.

**Open questions**

- All pricing. Lead price is described as lead cost plus filter cost, but no dollar figure, minimum deposit, or filter surcharge is published; pricing is quoted per agent by a sales representative.
- The Lead Credit Reason policy itself — the list of reasons a lead qualifies for credit, the submission window and the approval rate are not published on the public pages.
- Elite Program terms: volume threshold, pricing treatment and what the program actually includes.
- Whether live transfer calls carry any exclusivity commitment, and how the four-agent web lead cap interacts with territory.
- Which carriers subsidise QuoteWizard live transfers, and on what terms.
- Contract length, minimum spend, and whether an agent can pause or cancel without notice.
- Any independent verification of lead quality, close rate or return rate. Every performance figure on the site is vendor-published and is recorded here as unconfirmed.
- Whether the Udemy certificate program is still free and currently available, and whether completion is reported back to the agency owner.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_representative_checked
- disclosure: Research compiled from QuoteWizard's public agent-facing website only. No account, contract or pricing sheet was seen, and no QuoteWizard representative reviewed this record.
- note: Book mapping is editorial analysis, not partner-approved placement. Bind an existing organization and authorize release separately before this is loaded at runtime.

### 9. Wintrust Agent Finance

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://www.agentfinance.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/wintrust-agent-finance.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `wintrust-agent-finance`
- **Category:** Bank lending to insurance agency owners: agency acquisition, refinance, working capital and commercial real estate
- **Also known as:** Agent Finance, Wintrust, Lake Forest Bank and Trust Company, N.A.
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **entity** — Wintrust Agent Finance is a division of Lake Forest Bank and Trust Company, N.A., stated in the site footer on every page observed. It is a bank lender rather than a broker or a non-bank finance company. _(public_company_claim)_
- **positioning** — The company describes itself as serving the banking and financial needs of the insurance agent community, and states that as a bank it can offer more options and better terms than non-bank lenders. _(public_company_claim)_
- **product_lines** — Three product lines are published: a Term Loan, a Line of Credit, and Additional Services. Each has its own page and its own downloadable checklist and application. _(public_company_claim)_
- **term_loan_purpose** — The Term Loan is stated to fund agency acquisitions plus refinancing, consolidation and working capital needs. _(public_company_claim)_
- **term_loan_uses** — Four published Term Loan use cases: a new agency owner getting help purchasing their business; a current agency owner funding the purchase of an additional agency; refinancing existing business loans, business debt and seller financing; and working capital for marketing, staffing or office renovations. _(public_company_claim)_
- **seller_financing** — Seller financing is named explicitly as refinanceable debt, so an agency bought on a seller note can be moved onto bank paper afterwards. _(public_company_claim)_
- **term_loan_terms** — Published Term Loan terms: no application fees, and a fixed interest rate available for 3 to 12 years. Actual rates are not published on the site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **term_loan_minimum** — The published Term Loan requirement is a minimum agency size of $1.5 million in 12-month moving earned premium. This is the qualification gate for acquisition and refinance borrowing. _(public_company_claim)_
- **loc_purpose** — The Line of Credit is positioned as a consistent source of capital for existing agency owners funding business upgrades and improvements, rather than as acquisition money. _(public_company_claim)_
- **loc_terms** — The Line of Credit is published as revolving, with no application fees, and with line draws and repayments handled electronically. The page states that owners of large and small agencies may qualify. _(public_company_claim)_
- **loc_minimum** — The published Line of Credit requirement is a minimum agency size of $650,000 in 12-month moving earned premium, a materially lower bar than the $1.5 million Term Loan minimum. _(public_company_claim)_
- **premium_basis** — Both published qualification thresholds are expressed in 12-month moving earned premium, so agency size is measured by book premium rather than by revenue, commission or valuation multiple. _(public_company_claim)_
- **additional_services** — Additional Services lists business banking, SBA loans, office equipment lease, commercial real estate financing, business credit cards, and letters of credit. The home page inquiry form adds letters of credit to support office lease space. _(public_company_claim)_
- **credit_card_issuer** — Business credit cards are issued by a third party, not by Wintrust. The Additional Services page names Elan Financial Services as issuer and states Wintrust is not affiliated with it; the Resource Center page names First National Bank of Omaha. The two pages disagree and neither was confirmed with the company. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **application_paths** — The application routes borrowers into one of three self-selected paths: New Agent Purchase (a new agent purchasing an established agency), Existing Agent Purchase (an existing agent purchasing an established agency), and Existing Agent Refinance (refinancing existing agency loans and/or seeking a line of credit). _(public_company_claim)_
- **packet_types** — Two separate term-loan checklist and application packets are published for download: a Farmers Term Loan packet and an Other Agent Term Loan packet. Both are download, complete, print, sign and upload. _(public_company_claim)_
- **resource_center** — The Resource Center splits into Servicing, New Loan Application and Existing Borrower Reporting, and publishes a loan calculator for estimating monthly payments, carrying a disclaimer that its calculations are estimates and not financial, legal or tax advice. _(public_company_claim)_
- **referral_partner** — One referral partner is published in the Resource Center: G-Force Agency Consulting, listing Gary Chitwood as President. _(public_company_claim)_
- **leadership** — Published leadership is Lena Dawson, President, and Brian Klemstein, Group Senior Vice President. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact_routes** — Published contact routing: agentfinance@wintrust.com for application and underwriting, agentfinanceops@wintrust.com for closing and payment inquiries, and toll free 1-855-552-5626 for general loan questions. The office address is 9701 W. Higgins Rd., Ste. 330, Rosemont, Illinois 60018. _(public_company_claim)_
- **appointments** — The site offers scheduling an appointment with a banker as a front door alongside the online application, and an inquiry form that lets an agent pre-select which of the eight products they want information on. _(public_company_claim)_
- **underwriting_disclaimer** — Every page carries the statement that all loans and lines of credit are subject to underwriting and approval, and that contact with Wintrust Agent Finance creates no relationship of any kind. _(public_company_claim)_
- **allstate_preferred_claim** — The home page headline calls the company an Allstate agent preferred national lender with award-winning customer service. Both the carrier designation and the award claim are undated marketing statements and were not confirmed with Allstate or with the company. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **speed_and_rate_claims** — The site claims a streamlined approval process that gets an answer quickly, competitively priced agent loans, attractive loan terms and swift closings. These are comparative marketing claims with no published rate, timeline or benchmark behind them. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **affiliates** — Related Wintrust entities appear in the footnotes: residential lending is provided by Wintrust Mortgage, a division of Barrington Bank & Trust Company, N.A., NMLS #449042, and investment products are disclosed as not FDIC insured and not bank guaranteed. _(public_company_claim)_

**Sources**

- Welcome | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Term Loan | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/term-loan.html (seen 2026-09-09)
- Line of Credit | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/line-of-credit.html (seen 2026-09-09)
- Additional Services | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/additional-services.html (seen 2026-09-09)
- Resource Center | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/resource-center.html (seen 2026-09-09)
- New Loan Agent Application | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/new-loan-agent-application.html (seen 2026-09-09)
- Contact Us | Wintrust Agent Finance — https://www.agentfinance.com/contact-us.html (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| The Operating System | primary | An Office Built on Authenticity | Commercial real estate financing, office equipment lease and letters of credit to support office lease space are the money behind an office an owner actually controls, and the published premium thresholds tell an owner whether that move is financeable yet. The lender funds the room; it does not decide what the room is for. |
| The Sales Sequence | conditional | Recruit Recruit Recruit | Working capital for staffing is a named use of both the term loan and the line of credit, so the seat an owner cannot afford today can be financed. Conditional because a lender funds the hire and nothing else: the seat definition, the comp plan and the ramp still have to exist first, or the borrowed money just buys a longer runway to the same problem. |
| Making It Rain | conditional | Your Marketing Strategy | Marketing is a published use of working capital and the revolving line is built for uneven spend, which matters when a channel needs funding ahead of the premium it returns. Conditional because the bank supplies the capital and has no view on the channel, the creative or the return. |
| Growth Through Service | not_recommended | — | Nothing published by this partner touches service delivery, onboarding or retention. Financing an acquisition creates service load rather than solving it, and the lender has no capability on that side. |
| Commitment to Training | not_recommended | — | The published offering is lending and business banking. No curriculum, certification, scoring or coaching exists anywhere on the pages observed. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce a financing decision that was not made earlier. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Operating System_ exercise: Write down your 12-month moving earned premium, then say plainly which of the two published gates you clear today and what the office decision would cost.
- _The Operating System_ measure: 12-month moving earned premium against the $650,000 and $1.5 million thresholds, and total debt service as a share of monthly commission.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Price the seat you keep deferring, then decide whether it is a capital problem or a definition problem before you borrow anything.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Producer seats funded versus producer seats producing at the written floor by day 90.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Name the one channel you would fund on a line of credit, then write what it has to return before the second draw.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Marketing spend drawn on the line against premium written from that channel in the same period.

**On these resource pages**

- **Funding the Build** (/resources/funding-the-build)
  - Helps with: Lending and financing services built specifically for insurance agency owners.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves an acquisition, a buildout or a hire that needs capital ahead of the revenue it produces.

**Open questions**

- Interest rates, spreads, fees and closing costs. The site publishes only that the term loan carries a fixed rate for 3 to 12 years and that there are no application fees; every actual price is quoted privately and is deliberately absent here.
- Maximum loan size, advance rate against a book, loan-to-value on an acquisition, and any debt-service coverage or personal-guarantee requirement. Only the two minimum premium thresholds are published.
- Whether the $1.5 million and $650,000 premium minimums apply to the buyer's existing book, the target book, or the combined book after an acquisition.
- Which carriers beyond Farmers have their own application packet, and what the Other Agent Term Loan packet requires. The packets are PDFs that were not downloaded or read.
- Geographic lending footprint and licensing. The site says national but names no state list or exclusions.
- Typical time from application to funding. The site claims a quick answer and swift closings without publishing a number.
- Which entity currently issues the business credit card. The Additional Services page names Elan Financial Services and the Resource Center names First National Bank of Omaha.
- Any portfolio, volume, approval-rate or customer-count figure. None is published and none was asserted.
- The nature and terms of the G-Force Agency Consulting referral relationship.
- Wintrust Agent Finance's own org chart, capacity, assigned Formula contact and 2027 targets.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_representative_checked
- disclosure: Research compiled from seven public pages on agentfinance.com observed on 2026-09-09. No contact with the company was made and no account was verified.
- note: Approve the record and bind an existing organization before any runtime load. Book mapping is editorial analysis, not partner-approved placement. Lending terms change; re-verify before the expiry date.

### 10. Search Perfect

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://searchperfect.ca
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/search-perfect.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `search-perfect`
- **Category:** Search engine marketing agency — SEO, Google Ads and social media for insurance agents
- **Also known as:** Search Perfect Inc, SearchPerfect, SP Sign Lab
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (22 researched facts)**

- **founding** — Search Perfect Inc was founded in 2019 by Danielle Cameron, who the site states has worked in the SEO industry since 2006 and spent 12 years across two online marketing agencies before starting the company. _(public_company_claim)_
- **core_services** — The published service list is SEO, SEM, social media marketing, video marketing, YouTube marketing, graphic design and content marketing. The company summarises itself as a search engine marketing shop. _(public_company_claim)_
- **service_definitions** — The three headline services are defined on the page as: SEO — analysis, keyword research and link building; SEM — managing Google Ad accounts and developing strategies; Social Media — social media interactions and content strategy. _(public_company_claim)_
- **insurance_focus** — The founder's bio positions the firm's specialisation as SEO, SEM and social media for captive insurance agents, which is the only vertical named anywhere on the site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **effort_commitment** — The company states it spends a minimum of 6+ hours a month of work per client across SEO, SEM and social media, and that it does not use a streamlined approach because each business is treated as unique. _(public_company_claim)_
- **partner_status** — The site states Search Perfect is a Google Partner company, and the founder's bio describes the team as recognised as both a Google and Microsoft Advertising Partner. Partner status was not independently verified against Google's or Microsoft's directories. _(public_company_claim)_
- **seo_department** — SEO is run as a department. The SEO Departmental Manager leads a team of SEO strategists and owns market research, keyword research, competitive analysis and link building, and also contributes website and blog content. _(public_company_claim)_
- **client_relations** — A Director of Client Relations holds the client-facing relationship and also works inside the SEO department on market research, competitive analysis, keyword research and link building. _(public_company_claim)_
- **search_department** — A Director of Search owns Google Ads management, described as daily maintenance, troubleshooting and keyword research, and is stated to hold Google Search and Display certifications with ten years in SEM. _(public_company_claim)_
- **social_department** — Social media is handled by a named Social Media Manager plus dedicated social media specialists hired in 2023 and 2025, so social posting is staffed separately from the SEO and paid-search functions. _(public_company_claim)_
- **retention_role** — The company added a Retention and Engagement Specialist role in 2025, described as dedicated to client experience and problem solving — a service function separate from campaign delivery. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support_roles** — Supporting roles named on the site include an Administrative Manager who oversees day-to-day operations, client relations and financials, an Administrative Assistant, and a Brand Design Assistant who produces brand files and marketing materials. _(public_company_claim)_
- **call_coding** — One team member's bio states she began at Search Perfect in 2020 by coding calls, indicating the company reviews and classifies inbound client calls as part of its reporting work. No call-tracking product is described on the site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sign_lab** — SP Sign Lab is a sister operation led by a Vice President based in Calgary, Alberta, producing custom acrylic signs and physical print products — a physical-goods line alongside the digital marketing work. _(public_company_claim)_
- **distributed_team** — Team bios place staff in Calgary, Alberta and in Toronto and the Greater Toronto Area, Ontario, so delivery is a distributed Canadian team rather than a single office. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact_route** — The only published intake routes are a phone number, 888-268-2762, and a name/email/subject/message contact form on the single page. There is no client portal, dashboard, pricing page or self-serve signup on the site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **team_size_claim** — The founder's bio states she leads a dedicated team of 15 professionals. The site's own team section lists a different number of named people, and neither count is dated or confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **client_count_claim** — The founder's bio claims a track record working with over 300 insurance locations in Canada and the US. The claim is undated and was not confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **effort_comparison_claim** — The About section claims the 6+ hours a month per client is nearly five times the work most other companies spend on accounts. That is a comparative claim about competitors and was not confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **results_claim** — The site claims the team drives live trackable business results and transforms digital presence into measurable success. No metric, method or figure is published behind those statements. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **founding_year_conflict** — The About section dates the company's founding to 2019, while a team bio says that person has been with the company since its inception in 2018. The two published dates conflict and were not reconciled. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **sign_lab_site** — spsignlab.com loads a password gate reading OPENING SOON. Whether that storefront is operated by Search Perfect Inc, and whether sign products can currently be ordered, was not confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Search Perfect — Welcome, services and team — https://searchperfect.ca/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Search Perfect — About Us section — https://searchperfect.ca/#about (seen 2026-09-09)
- Search Perfect — Get in touch section — https://searchperfect.ca/#contact (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Making It Rain | primary | Your Marketing Strategy | SEO, Google Ads management and social posting are the whole offering, which is exactly the channel work this session asks an owner to decide on. The agency executes channels; the owner still chooses which channels to fund and what a lead is worth. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Position Definition & Focus % | A named Retention and Engagement Specialist plus call coding means someone outside the agency is listening to how inbound calls are handled. Conditional because that is the vendor's own client-service function, not a service standard installed in your agency. |
| The Sales Sequence | conditional | Accountability | Call coding produces a record of what came in, which is raw material for holding a producer to a follow-up standard. Conditional because nothing published scores the call itself or defines the sequence. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: List the channels you pay for today, then write which of them you could name last month's cost per quoted household on.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Cost per lead and per quoted household by channel, month over month.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Ask who in your office answers a marketing-generated call first, and write what they are required to say.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Inbound marketing calls answered live versus missed, by hour of day.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Pick last month's paid-search calls and decide the one number you would hold a producer to on them.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Percentage of marketing-sourced calls that reached a quote.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Search engine optimization and organic search visibility for agency websites.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves being found by the shoppers already searching for insurance in your market.

**Open questions**

- All pricing. No price, package, retainer or minimum spend is published anywhere on the site; engagement terms are quoted privately.
- What the agency has to supply. Onboarding inputs, brand assets, website access, Google Business Profile access, ad account ownership and approval steps are not described.
- Contract terms, minimum commitment and cancellation. Nothing published.
- Reporting. No dashboard, report cadence, call-tracking product or attribution method is described, despite call coding being mentioned in a staff bio.
- Whether the agency owns or the client owns the Google Ads account and the ad data at the end of an engagement.
- Whether US insurance agents are served directly or only Canadian agents. The company is Canadian and claims US locations, but no US office or entity is published.
- Carrier or captive-brand compliance handling for ad copy and social posts. Not addressed on the site.
- The site is a single page. There are no service, pricing, case study, blog or client-portal pages to corroborate the offering beyond the homepage.
- Whether SP Sign Lab is part of Search Perfect Inc, what it sells and whether it is currently trading.

### 11. Ricochet360

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://ricochet360.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/ricochet360.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `ricochet360`
- **Category:** Cloud phone system, CRM and lead management, auto dialer and marketing automation for sales teams including insurance agencies
- **Also known as:** Ricochet 360, Ricochet, Ricochet360 CRM
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (26 researched facts)**

- **category** — Ricochet360 is an all-in-one cloud phone system, CRM and lead management system, dialer and marketing automation platform, sold to agencies, brokers and sales teams. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact** — The published address is 1343 Main Street Suite 205, Sarasota FL 34236, with sales at (866) 678-1288 and support at (888) 579-9559. _(public_company_claim)_
- **verticals** — Insurance is one of six named industry solutions alongside mortgage, solar, call centre, merchant cash advance and education, and it has its own Insurance CRM page. _(public_company_claim)_
- **plans_annual** — Five plans are published with annual per-seat pricing: Shuttle $20, Cruiser $35, Booster $85, Explorer $125 and Galaxy $165 per user per month paid annually. Explorer and Galaxy are shown discounted from $150 and $198. _(public_company_claim)_
- **plans_monthly** — Month-to-month per-seat pricing is published as Shuttle $35, Cruiser $45, Booster $102, Explorer $150 and Galaxy $198. _(public_company_claim)_
- **minutes** — Included minutes per user are 500, 1000, 1500 and 2000 on the four lower plans and unlimited on Galaxy. Overage is $0.05 per minute and SMS is $0.02 per message on Explorer and Galaxy. _(public_company_claim)_
- **call_caps** — All plans except Galaxy cap outbound at 300 calls per day and 6,000 calls per month. Galaxy is uncapped. _(public_company_claim)_
- **line_pricing** — Additional lines are published at $3.00 per local line and $5.00 per toll-free line. _(public_company_claim)_
- **spam_guru_pricing** — Spam Guru Pro is priced per number per month at $2.00 for a 10:1 call cap ratio, $3.00 for 25:1 and $4.00 for 50:1, with annual plans also available. _(public_company_claim)_
- **tier_gating** — Feature access is tiered. Local presence, caller ID masking, Spam Guru, dialling technology, agent and lead performance reporting start at Booster. Email and text automation, integrations, SMS and email reporting and gamification reporting start at Explorer. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support_gating** — Support is tiered the same way: a ticketing system and a customer service line start at Booster, live Zoom support at Explorer, and premium support at Galaxy. A virtual training course and a YouTube user guide are on every plan. _(public_company_claim)_
- **spam_guru_function** — Spam Guru generates a Risk Rating from 0 to 100 aggregated from multiple sources, buckets numbers as Low, Moderate or High risk, alerts subscribers automatically when a number goes High, and lets them buy replacement numbers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **call_limits** — Spam Guru also lets a customer set an outbound calling limit per number per day to reduce the chance of a number being flagged or blocked. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dnc** — A DNC scrubber lets customers build custom filters by lead age, lead status including do-not-call, and lead vendor, and exclude numbers on the Federal DNC list. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ai_features** — Two AI features are published: AI Auto Categorization for lead tracking and call classification, and AI Note Taking that listens to calls and writes structured summaries with key points, action items and outcomes into the CRM. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dialpad** — The all-in-one dialpad puts calling, texting, emailing, note taking and lead detail in a single interface, and custom call scripts can be embedded directly in the dialer for objection handling, qualification and compliance protocols. _(public_company_claim)_
- **crm_mechanics** — CRM features include automated call cadence at set intervals, real-time lead capture from any form or partner through a RESTful API, and automated lead assignment by skill level, licensing, tags and availability. _(public_company_claim)_
- **campaigns** — Leads can be segmented and worked as outreach campaigns by lead age, status, vendor, state and tags. _(public_company_claim)_
- **automation_scope** — Marketing automation covers drip email and SMS triggered by lead status and behaviour, status-driven contact points across the funnel, and two-factor authentication one-time passwords. _(public_company_claim)_
- **a2p** — The company states it is A2P 10DLC compliant and operates a global compliance engine covering in-country legislation across borders, positioned as improving SMS deliverability. _(public_company_claim)_
- **insurance_features** — Features listed on the Insurance CRM page include a gamification dashboard with real-time leaderboards, call recording and monitoring with talk time and abandonment metrics, local caller ID, one-click Perfect Voicemail, ringless voicemail via third-party providers, lead distribution and tagging, direct mail management, referral tracking forms and private managed cloud hosting. _(public_company_claim)_
- **hosting** — The platform is described as cloud-hosted on Amazon infrastructure with a redundant setup, and a private managed cloud deployment is offered as an option. _(public_company_claim)_
- **speed_claim** — The site claims the phone system connects agents to leads in under one second using patent-pending technology and that it is the fastest commercially available dialer. This is an unverified vendor performance claim. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **uptime_claim** — The site claims 99.9% uptime on its redundant cloud-hosted system. No SLA document or status page was published on the pages observed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **workflow_claim** — The Insurance CRM page claims teams automate 95% of their workflow. No methodology or date accompanies the figure. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **reviews_claim** — The homepage cites 1,000+ Google Reviews. This is an undated marketing count and was not verified against the review source. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Take the one call your team should run, paste it into the script field, then name the two moments you would score a recording on.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Recorded calls reviewed per producer per week, and the share that followed the embedded script.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Write the one service touch that should never be missed, then decide the status change that would trigger it.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Automated service touches delivered on time, as a share of those the rule should have fired.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name one process that currently only works because you are in the room, and decide whether it can be encoded as a routing or campaign rule.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether the routing and cadence rules run correctly for a full week with the owner out of the office.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one recorded call per producer this week and write the single correction you would make from it.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Recordings reviewed per producer per week, and corrections that changed a later call.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Draw where a new lead goes in the first sixty seconds, then decide the rule that routes it there without you.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Time from lead arrival to first dial, and leads worked per source per producer.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: CRM, dialing, lead workflows, automation, follow-up and sales activity visibility.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves speed-to-lead and working the opportunity you already bought before it goes cold.
- **Formula Sales Sequence Resources** (/resources/sales-sequence)
  - Helps with: CRM, calling, lead workflows, automation, follow-up and sales activity visibility.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves speed-to-lead, consistent follow-up or a more disciplined producer workflow.
- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: CRM workflows, activity visibility, automation and repeatable follow-up processes.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves making day-to-day sales activity visible and turning follow-up into a managed system.

**Open questions**

- What the published seat prices exclude. Minutes overage, SMS per message, extra lines and Spam Guru Pro are all priced separately, and no setup, onboarding, porting or minimum-seat terms are published.
- Which systems Ricochet360 integrates with. Integrations are gated to the Explorer plan and a RESTful API is named, but no integration list, agency management system or comparative rater is published.
- Whether Spam Guru Pro is the same product as the Spam Guru feature included from the Booster plan, or a paid upgrade to it. The pricing page lists both without distinguishing them.
- Contract length, cancellation terms and what happens to recordings and data after cancellation. None is published.
- Company ownership, founding date, leadership and headcount. No about or team page exists on the site.
- Any security certification, SLA document or public status page backing the 99.9% uptime claim.
- How the AI features handle call data: which model or vendor, where transcripts are stored, and whether recordings are used for training. Not published.
- Whether the insurance vertical is built for captive or independent agencies, and which carriers or raters its insurance customers typically use.
- The 1,000+ Google Reviews, sub-second connect speed, 95% workflow automation and 99.9% uptime figures are all undated vendor claims and were not independently verified.

### 12. Arbeit

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://arbeitsoftware.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/arbeit.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `arbeit`
- **Category:** Cloud VoIP phone systems, outbound dialers and caller ID reputation monitoring
- **Also known as:** Arbeit Software, Arbeit Click, Arbeit Dialer, Arbeit Voice, Numberlab
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Matt Robinson
- Vimeo id: 1223367402
- Contact: Email Matt → mailto:matt@arbeitsoftware.com
- Shown as: matt@arbeitsoftware.com · arbeitsoftware.com
- Note: Matt invited agencies to email him or request a meeting through Arbeit. He can also run a free report showing how their phone numbers are labeled.

**What we know (28 researched facts)**

- **founding** — Arbeit was founded in 2013 by two collection agency owners who were frustrated by the call centre technology available to them. Arbeit Dialer, a voice broadcast dialer, was the first product and the rest of the suite grew from it. _(public_company_claim)_
- **team** — Arbeit is a fully remote team with roots in Buffalo, New York and Belo Horizonte, Brazil. Published leadership names Alex Villafranca as CEO and Alonzo Rainero and Athena Lisboa as co-founders. _(public_company_claim)_
- **market** — The company's stated core market is collection agencies and businesses across the United States. The about page frames the mission as revolutionising how collection agencies communicate with consumers, so insurance agencies are an adjacent market rather than the primary one. _(public_company_claim)_
- **products** — There are four products: Arbeit Click, a TCPA-compliant manual click dialing solution; Arbeit Dialer, a voice broadcast and press-1 dialer; Arbeit Voice, a cloud VoIP phone system; and Numberlab, caller ID reputation monitoring and remediation. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_mechanic** — Arbeit Click is explicitly not an auto-dialer. Agents manually click each call, listen, decide whether the answer is a live human or voicemail, and decide whether to leave a message. The company describes three layers of human intervention designed to eliminate the three-second pause. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_overflow** — Click lets an administrator set how many numbers each agent can call at once, and routes overflow to the next available agent when multiple contacts answer at the same time. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_features** — Other Click features are warm transfers, live call monitoring, advanced search and reporting. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_pricing** — Arbeit Click is published at $50 per agent per month on a one-year term or $75 per agent per month on a monthly plan, in both cases with usage starting at $0.05 per minute. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_includes** — Every Click plan is stated to include unlimited support, free training, four years of recording storage, real-time analytics, unlimited campaigns and unlimited call blending. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_integrations** — Click integrates with web-based CRMs, and the named examples are Simplicity, Beam, SMAX, CollectionsMax, Interprose and Balto. These are debt collection systems, not insurance agency management systems. _(public_company_claim)_
- **integration_process** — The company states it contacts a prospect's other software providers before and after implementation to determine whether integration is possible, and will say whether it is possible before any commitment is made. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dialer_pricing** — Arbeit Dialer is billed per connected minute: $0.03 per minute under 10,000 minutes, $0.025 per minute between 50,000 and 65,000 minutes, and a custom rate above 65,000 minutes. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dialer_billing** — Dialer billing runs weekly for the previous week's usage, charged per connected minute with a 30-second minimum and then 6-second intervals, which the company says exists to maintain traffic quality with its tier 1 carriers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **dialer_features** — Dialer features are press-1 campaigns, SIP trunking, text-to-speech voicemail generation, voice broadcasting, saved smart call lists, custom messaging and unlimited scripts with no calls-per-second cap. _(public_company_claim)_
- **voice_pricing** — Arbeit Voice is priced per extension per month in three bands. Starter, 1-9 extensions: $29.99 on a three-year contract, $32.49 on one year, $39.99 with no contract. Growth, 10-19: $24.99, $27.49, $34.99. Enterprise, 20+: $19.99, $22.49, $29.99. _(public_company_claim)_
- **voice_features** — Arbeit Voice includes unlimited call recording and call reporting at no extra cost, a mobile app so a cell phone doubles as the business line, conference access, call queues and an operator console. Arbeit ports existing numbers and ships pre-programmed phones. _(public_company_claim)_
- **numberlab_function** — Numberlab is described as patent pending. It checks how a business's numbers are labelled on real devices across carriers, registers the business as verified with call labelling providers, remediates mislabelled numbers, and runs automated daily or weekly checks with reports showing which numbers changed. _(public_company_claim)_
- **numberlab_pricing** — Numberlab is published at $99 per month plus $1 per number for the Basic plan with weekly checks and reports, and $199 per month plus $2 per number for the Premium plan with daily checks and reports. Discounts are stated for over 200 numbers, and annual and month-to-month terms are both offered. _(public_company_claim)_
- **numberlab_includes** — Both Numberlab plans include number registration and negative label remediation; the difference between them is check and report frequency. _(public_company_claim)_
- **insurance_article** — Arbeit publishes an article dated August 15, 2025 specifically about insurance agencies, attributing spam labelling to multiple or rotating outbound lines, recycled numbers from businesses with poor calling practices, and scripts that sound like cold calls. It positions Numberlab as the remedy. _(public_company_claim)_
- **onboarding** — Arbeit states it sets everything up at no extra cost with no long-term contracts required, and offers a seven-day free trial on Arbeit Click with no credit card required. _(public_company_claim)_
- **process** — The published engagement path has three steps: meet a product expert who will say whether a solution is or is not a fit, then take a demo, a free trial or both, then switch. Discovery calls are booked through Calendly. _(public_company_claim)_
- **support** — Support is an in-house team, backed by a Zendesk help centre, a public product status page and a screen-sharing link. Support is stated to be included rather than charged. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact** — The published phone number is (844) 444-4401. _(public_company_claim)_
- **click_volume_claim** — Arbeit claims Click makes 250+ outbound calls per agent per hour. This is an unverified vendor performance claim with no methodology published. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **dialer_rate_claim** — Arbeit claims the Dialer increases connection rates up to 18% and beats the competition every time. Unverified vendor outcome claim. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **numberlab_claims** — Numberlab's page claims 99% network coverage and that 80% of consumers will not answer a call labelled SPAM or SCAM. The 80% figure is attributed elsewhere on the site to a Pew Research study of unknown-number calls, which is not the same measurement. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **third_party_stats** — The insurance article cites Pew Research that 80% of U.S. adults ignore calls from unknown numbers, and Hiya's 2024 State of the Call report that answer rates can drop by as much as 90% once a number is flagged. These are third-party figures repeated by the vendor. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

- Arbeit — About Us — https://arbeitsoftware.com/about-us/ (seen 2026-09-09)

Supporting rather than primary because it encodes call flow only, not the processes around it. |

Conditional because Arbeit publishes no sales curriculum, no scoring rubric and no role play capability. |

Numberlab makes caller reputation visible on real devices and remediates it, which protects the contact rate on opportunities the agency already paid for. It generates no opportunities of its own. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Name the daily dial count one seat owes you, then decide who listens to one of those calls each week.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Dials per agent per hour, and recorded calls actually reviewed per week.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Write the one service message worth sending to your whole book, then decide what happens when someone presses 1.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Answer and press-1 response rate on a service broadcast, measured against the list size.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Draw where an inbound call goes when the first person does not pick up, and decide who owns that queue.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether inbound calls route correctly for a full week without anyone intervening manually.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one recorded call per producer this week and write the single correction it earns.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Recorded calls reviewed per producer per week.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: List every outbound number your agency dials from, then decide who checks monthly how those numbers are labelled.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Share of your outbound numbers carrying a negative carrier label, checked on a set cadence.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Calling technology, activity visibility, recorded conversations and outbound sales workflows.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves actually reaching the opportunity sitting in your system.
- **Formula Sales Sequence Resources** (/resources/sales-sequence)
  - Helps with: Calling technology, activity visibility, recorded conversations and outbound sales workflows.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves producer call activity, follow-up discipline or coaching from real conversations.

**Open questions**

- Whether Arbeit integrates with any insurance agency management system, rater or CRM. The named integrations are all debt collection systems, and the company's answer is to contact the prospect's provider case by case.
- How many insurance agencies Arbeit actually serves. Its published market, case studies and testimonials are collection agencies and small businesses; the only insurance-specific material is one blog post.
- Numberlab's carrier coverage detail behind the 99% network coverage claim: which carriers and labelling providers are covered, and what remediation actually involves.
- Arbeit Voice setup and hardware costs. Pre-programmed phones and porting are described as included in setup at no extra cost, but the hardware cost itself is not published.
- The Arbeit Dialer price between 10,000 and 50,000 minutes. The published tiers skip that range, and the top tier above 65,000 minutes is unpriced.
- Whether Arbeit Click carries a per-seat minimum, and what the one-year term costs to break.
- Whether Numberlab is sold standalone to non-Arbeit customers or requires one of the calling products. It has its own subdomain and its own pricing, which suggests standalone, but this is not stated.
- TCPA and A2P posture for insurance-specific outbound. Click is described as TCPA compliant for collections use; nothing addresses insurance solicitation rules or state-level requirements.
- Company size, ownership and funding. The about page names three leaders and a remote structure but publishes no headcount or ownership.
- The 250+ calls per agent per hour and up to 18% connection rate figures are undated vendor claims with no published methodology.

- **Tier:** Silver
- **Website:** https://postpros.com/insurance
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/post-pros.svg`
- **Resource-page id:** `post-pros`
- **Category:** Direct mail and digital marketing agency — personalised home insurance mailers plus social and paid ads
- **Also known as:** The Post Pros, PostPros
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **founding** — Post Pros was founded in 2023 by two cousins with backgrounds in insurance, marketing and data. The site names Ryan Francis as Founder and CEO. _(public_company_claim)_
- **location** — The company is headquartered in Spring, Texas and states it serves businesses across the country. Contact is 281.801.7007 and info@thepostpros.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **service_lines** — The published services are direct mail, social media, digital ads, custom print and design services. Direct mail is the core; digital is positioned as the surround. _(public_company_claim)_
- **full_service** — Direct mail is sold as full-service and done-for-you: mail design, targeted list building, data processing, printing and mailing fulfilment are all handled by Post Pros rather than split across vendors. _(public_company_claim)_
- **variable_data** — Personalisation is done with variable data printing, customising names, details, quotes and messages so each piece differs by recipient rather than running one template. _(public_company_claim)_
- **insurance_personalisation** — The insurance mailer is described as carrying a Google Street View photo of the recipient's own home, a "Hi [Name]," greeting with their local city, and the agent's photo and local office number. _(public_company_claim)_
- **envelope_design** — The mail piece is deliberately built to get opened: unmarked no-logo envelopes, no preview window, a real stamp and security tint. _(public_company_claim)_
- **response_mechanics** — Response paths are a QR code that opens a pre-filled email or text, and call routing to the agent's direct line. The stated design principle is no clunky forms. _(public_company_claim)_
- **targeting** — Insurance targeting is by ZIP codes the agent is competitive in, with defined filters described as targeting, value, renewals and age. More generally, prospect lists are built from advanced targeting tools and can key on new movers, specific ZIP codes or a custom list the client supplies. _(public_company_claim)_
- **process** — The published process is four steps: Discovery (define audience and campaign goals with the client), Onboarding (a simple form where Post Pros learns the business), Setup (their team builds the mail piece) and Campaign Start (they prepare, print and mail). _(public_company_claim)_
- **custom_templates** — Templates are custom per agency rather than shared: the site states mail is designed to the agency's own brand, audience and goals, across home, auto, life or all three. _(public_company_claim)_
- **formats** — Available formats are postcards, letters in envelopes, brochures, flyers and custom formats, with Post Pros recommending the format based on industry, goals, budget and audience. _(public_company_claim)_
- **quote_matrix** — A Quote Matrix intake page exists, opening with agency name, email and state before continuing. It is the mechanism by which the quoted premium shown on a mailer is set up. _(public_company_claim)_
- **job_upload** — The site carries Submit Your Job and Upload pages plus an agent registration page, so an existing client can hand off print or mail jobs and files directly rather than by email. _(public_company_claim)_
- **digital_side** — On the digital side the company creates, manages and optimises paid and organic content on Facebook, Instagram and TikTok, and runs retargeting aimed both at website visitors and at prospects from a direct mail campaign. _(public_company_claim)_
- **industries** — Named industries served are insurance, real estate, home services, medical and dental practices, retail, auto shops, salons and non-profits. Insurance has its own dedicated page. _(public_company_claim)_
- **positioning** — The company positions itself as an outsourced marketing department — "brainpower without payroll" — for businesses that want big-agency output without hiring a team. _(public_company_claim)_
- **values** — The published values spell GROW: Generate, Respect, Optimize, Win. The stated mission is to simplify marketing and get the right message in front of the right people on paper and on screen. _(public_company_claim)_
- **discovery_call** — Intake is a free 30-minute discovery call, booked from the site. There is no self-serve purchase path or published price list. _(public_company_claim)_
- **allstate_design_claim** — The insurance page states the program was designed by an Allstate agent and is "Proven to work." Neither the designer's identity nor the proof was published or confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **open_rate_claim** — The site claims 90% of direct mail gets opened and that mail volume has dropped by 50% since 2008, so a mailer stands out more. Both carry an asterisk on the page with no source given. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **outcome_claims** — The insurance page asserts that mail responders are higher quality than internet leads, that responses are exclusive to the agent, that older homeowners retain better, and that close rate increases. These are argument, not measured results, and none were confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **volume_claims** — Counters for Direct Mail Pieces Delivered and Total Clicks Across Platforms render as an unpopulated zero on every page observed. The About page separately claims millions of highly personalised pieces mailed per month. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **case_claims** — The About page claims doubling appointments at a single-location dental office and generating thousands of leads for a nationwide insurance provider. No client, period or figure is named. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **founder_credentials** — The founder's bio claims a social audience of over 500,000, features in Forbes, CNBC, Today.com and Bankrate, and national bestselling personal finance author status. None were verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
