### 27. Melon Local

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://melonlocal.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/melon-local.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `melon-local`
- **Category:** Digital marketing agency for local businesses — local SEO, paid search, social, reputation and insurance lead generation
- **Also known as:** Melon Local Advertising, Melon, Melon Max, Melon University
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (25 researched facts)**

- **company_shape** — Melon Local is a digital marketing agency for local businesses, based at 6760 Surrey St, Las Vegas, NV 89119, reachable by call or text on 702-723-2792. Every published client testimonial is attributed to an insurance agent. _(public_company_claim)_
- **service_lines** — Six service lines are published: Strategic SEO & GEO, AI-Powered Paid Search, Personalized Social Media, Review Management, Customized Brand Building, and Melon Max live transfer calls and internet leads. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_contracts** — The company states there are no contracts — "No Contracts, Just Results. We earn your business with performance, not paperwork." _(public_company_claim)_
- **csm_model** — Delivery is built around a named Client Success Manager per account, with monthly check-ins and a client-accessible dashboard for tracking data and progress. Social media has its own dedicated Social Success Manager. _(public_company_claim)_
- **seo_offering** — The Local SEO offering is: online business listing management with consistent name, address and phone across dozens of directories including Google and Yelp, Google Business Profile optimization, GEO-focused content for AI search visibility, original Google product and language tiles, GBP posts, Google and Yelp review response management, monthly CSM check-ins and a progress dashboard. _(public_company_claim)_
- **paid_offering** — The Paid Search offering is: Google and Microsoft search ads management, advertising for nearly any product line in any language, AI-driven Performance Max campaigns, Google Local Service Ads for local targeting, standard, call-only, multi-state and custom campaign types, and Google Display (YouTube) and Facebook ad presence building. _(public_company_claim)_
- **paid_flexibility** — Paid search is described as continuously refined rather than launched and left, and the company states that if a client changes their mind or their budget, the agency adjusts. _(public_company_claim)_
- **social_offering** — The Social Media offering is: content across Facebook, Instagram and LinkedIn, consistent posting on all platforms, monthly campaign reports and success tracking, optional boosted posts for reach, a dedicated Social Success Manager and options for personalized content. _(public_company_claim)_
- **social_content_types** — Named social content types are educational videos, team highlights, office outings and recruiting posts, produced with a stated blend of AI insight and human creative rather than a template calendar. _(public_company_claim)_
- **review_offering** — The Review Management offering is: Google and Yelp review response management, ongoing reputation monitoring and proactive engagement, strategic outreach to encourage more positive reviews, monthly performance tracking and a dedicated Client Success Manager. _(public_company_claim)_
- **max_shape** — Melon Max is the lead product: real-time live transfer calls with verified, pre-qualified prospects, plus verified internet leads sold in qualification tiers, with AI-enhanced targeting and exclusive ad placements. It has its own contact line, 702-891-4872. _(public_company_claim)_
- **max_auto_pricing** — Auto internet leads are published at plain per-lead prices in four tiers: Standard $4.00, Preferred $10.80, Premium $12.00 and Premium Plus $14.40. _(public_company_claim)_
- **max_home_pricing** — Home internet leads are published at two tiers: Premium $14.40 and Premium Plus $16.80. _(public_company_claim)_
- **max_auto_filters** — Auto lead tiers are defined by published filters. Every tier excludes current carrier policyholders. Preferred adds currently insured, fewer than two incidents, valid license and no SR-22. Premium adds insured 6+ months and homeowner. Premium Plus adds insured 1+ years, 2+ cars and fewer than one incident. _(public_company_claim)_
- **max_home_filters** — Home lead tiers exclude current carrier policyholders and exclude duplex, apartment and mobile home property types. Premium Plus additionally excludes prospects with claims in the past three years. _(public_company_claim)_
- **university** — Melon University is a free-to-join community and learning platform — account setup is three steps using an email address — carrying expert-led workshops, community discussion and networking. Some past masterclass recordings are sold separately. _(public_company_claim)_
- **university_content** — Observed Melon University sessions include planning and goal setting, measuring performance and accountability led by a senior consultant from MAP, hiring models led by a producing insurance agent, live product demos, and a three-part sales masterclass series with a Las Vegas insurance agent covering life, health and bundling. _(public_company_claim)_
- **google_partner** — The company states it is a Google Partner and, on the paid search page, a Google Premier Partner with access to Google's newest AI paid search tools. The site links to a Google Partners directory listing. _(public_company_claim)_
- **intake_form** — Intake is a scheduled demo or a form asking for name, cell phone, contact preference, email, whether the agent is a current, former or prospective client, and which services they want. There is no self-serve signup or published onboarding checklist. _(public_company_claim)_
- **brand_page_duplication** — The Brand Building page's "Our Brand Building Offerings" list is word-for-word the Review Management offerings list. What brand building actually delivers beyond reputation work is therefore not established by the site. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **volume_claims** — Published volume claims include 24M+ search impressions annually, 17M+ map views per year, 3.5M+ clicks, calls and conversions per year, 100K+ business listings managed, 60K+ posts created, 95K+ engagements and 1.7M+ social impressions. None are dated or attributed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **paid_performance_claims** — The paid search page claims $1.4M+ in monthly ad spend, 79M+ impressions generated monthly, a 30% average conversion rate, and that the company ranks in the top three percent of Google advertisers. None were confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **third_party_stats** — The site cites unsourced third-party statistics — 50% of US consumers using Google Search and Maps for local businesses, AI ad targeting raising conversion about 35%, AI-driven creative boosting engagement up to 25%, 77% and 71% social purchase and recommendation figures. No citations are given. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **testimonial_outcomes** — Agent testimonials assert increased leads, more call-ins from Google, lower cost per click and shifted ROI. These are individual customer statements, undated and unverified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **awards** — The homepage links to a Top Workplaces Nevada listing and a post about an Inc. Rocky Mountain regional ranking of No. 15. Neither was opened or confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Put the published lead prices next to your own cost per quoted household, then decide which channel you would cut first.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Cost per lead, per quoted household and per bound policy, by channel and by lead tier.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Decide who picks up a live transfer at 4:45pm on a Friday, and what happens if nobody does.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Live transfers answered, quoted and bound, per producer, per week.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick one Melon U session, assign it to one seat, and write the date you will check what changed.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Sessions completed per seat, and one behaviour changed per session.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Read your three most recent negative reviews out loud and name the internal process each one is actually about.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: New reviews per month and average rating, tracked next to service complaints logged internally.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Name who inside your agency owns the vendor relationship and attends the monthly check-in. If the answer is you, decide who it should be.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Monthly check-ins held with a non-owner attending, out of twelve.

**On these resource pages**

- **Making It Rain Resources** (/resources/making-it-rain)
  - Helps with: Local SEO, paid search, social media, review management and live transfer calls for local businesses.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves building local demand and a review presence around your agency.

**Open questions**

- Pricing for SEO, paid search, social media, review management and brand building. Only Melon Max per-lead prices are published; every managed service is quoted after a demo.
- Whether the published lead prices are per lead outright, and whether minimums, volume commitments or setup fees apply.
- Live transfer call pricing. Only internet lead prices are published.
- Lead exclusivity. The pages say leads are pre-qualified and verified but do not state whether an internet lead is sold to one agent or several.
- Which carriers or captive brands the agency is cleared to advertise for, and how carrier ad-copy compliance is handled.
- What the agency has to supply at onboarding — brand assets, Google Business Profile access, ad account ownership, approval workflow and turnaround expectations are not described.
- Who owns the Google Ads account, the listings and the review-response history if the relationship ends. No-contract terms make this material.
- What the client dashboard actually shows, how often it refreshes, and whether it reports bound premium or only clicks and calls.
- Whether Melon University access is included with a service subscription or open to anyone, and what the paid masterclass recordings cost.
- What Brand Building delivers. Its published offerings list duplicates the Review Management list.

### 28. Slide Insurance

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://slideinsurance.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/slide-insurance.svg`
- **Resource-page id:** `slide-insurance`
- **Category:** Technology-enabled coastal residential property insurance carrier distributing through independent agents
- **Also known as:** Slide, Slide Insurance Holdings, Inc., Slide Specialty Insurance Company, Slide Insurance Company
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (23 researched facts)**

- **what** — Slide is a technology-enabled residential property insurance carrier focused on coastal homeowners, describing itself as revolutionising every facet of property insurance. _(public_company_claim)_
- **founding** — Slide was founded in 2021 in Tampa, Florida by Bruce and Shannon Lucas and launched operations in February 2022. Bruce Lucas is Founder and CEO at the holding company level and Shannon Lucas is President and Chief Operating Officer. _(public_company_claim)_
- **products** — The product set is homeowners insurance, condominium insurance, landlord insurance and commercial residential insurance. _(public_company_claim)_
- **states** — Slide lists Florida, South Carolina, California, Rhode Island, New York and New Jersey as states served. In California, Slide Specialty Insurance Company is a surplus lines insurer that is not licensed or admitted by the California Department of Insurance, so California risks are placed only through a properly licensed California surplus lines broker. _(public_company_claim)_
- **distribution** — Slide distributes through independent agents and states it is looking to partner with top-performing agents. Agents request an appointment through an Agent Appointment Request form on the agent page. _(public_company_claim)_
- **violet** — Violet is Slide's proprietary agent platform, described as built from the ground up alongside agents. Violet handles Florida quote and servicing after 1/29/24, with South Carolina listed as coming soon. _(public_company_claim)_
- **platform_matrix** — Agents work across more than one system: Violet for Florida, a Legacy Platform for Florida DP3-only quoting plus servicing and for South Carolina SIC HO3/HO6 quote and service, a separate Commercial Platform for Florida quote and service, and single sign-on for Rhode Island quote and service. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_workflow_claim** — Slide states the agent workflow uses accurately pre-filled fields and quick underwriting responses so agent time goes to growing the book rather than administration, and positions itself as a growth and support engine rather than only a carrier partner. _(public_company_claim)_
- **field_team** — Slide publishes a named agent-facing team: a Chief Revenue Officer, a Director of Agency Experience, a Director of Sales, an Agency Training and Communications Manager, Florida and South Carolina Territory Sales Managers, a Strategic Partnerships Manager, a Commercial Territory Sales Manager, and an inside sales team. _(public_company_claim)_
- **endorsements** — Published coverage customisations include additional living expenses, other structures limits, jewelry and other valuables, a hurricane screen enclosure endorsement at replacement cost, animal liability, and water backup and overflow. _(public_company_claim)_
- **commercial_residential** — The Commercial Residential Program covers homeowner association insurance and condominium association insurance, aimed at Florida property owners, boards of directors and property managers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **citizens_takeout** — Slide participates in the Citizens Depopulation Program. Slide states its takeout offer closely matches the policyholder's current Citizens coverages, that the policy renews automatically with nothing required from the policyholder at assumption, and that coverage can be customised with the agent at the renewal offer. _(public_company_claim)_
- **citizens_eligibility_rule** — Slide states that if a private-market renewal offer is more than 20 percent above the Citizens renewal premium, the policyholder remains eligible for Citizens and has until the date on the Offer Form to register a choice to stay. Slide also states it notifies the mortgage company directly and sends the invoice there if the mortgage company pays the premium. _(public_company_claim)_
- **citizens_comparison_docs** — Slide publishes a Personal Lines Coverage Comparison Worksheet and a Commercial Residential Worksheet showing the differences between Citizens and Slide coverage, and directs the policyholder to their agent to evaluate options. _(public_company_claim)_
- **rating** — Slide carries a Demotech Financial Stability Rating of A (Exceptional) and states its reinsurance program exceeds regulatory requirements. _(public_company_claim)_
- **procast** — Slide has built proprietary systems that put data, analytics and automation into underwriting and risk evaluation. One named internal capability, ProCast, applies data-driven models and risk management algorithms to produce predictive insights used by Slide personnel for property risk, underwriting and portfolio management decisions. _(public_company_claim)_
- **claims** — Claims are reported through an online first-notice-of-loss portal or by calling (866) 230-3758. Slide runs a pre-vetted emergency contractor referral line at (833) 854-3440 and instructs policyholders to photograph damage for the claims examiner. _(public_company_claim)_
- **self_service** — Policyholders get a policy access portal, online payments, a Storm Center, and iOS and Android mobile apps, which Slide positions as self-service alongside live support. _(public_company_claim)_
- **public_company** — Slide operates an investor relations site at ir.slideinsurance.com with a news releases section, indicating a public reporting posture. Slide Insurance is a registered trademark of Slide Insurance Holdings, Inc. _(public_company_claim)_
- **sponsorships** — Slide describes itself as the proud home insurance partner of the Tampa Bay Buccaneers and the Tampa Bay Lightning. _(public_company_claim)_
- **agent_network_size** — Slide states it offers home, condo and commercial residential products through a network of over 5,000 agents in Florida and South Carolina, and describes itself as one of the largest coastal insurance companies in the country. These counts are undated marketing claims and were not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **assessment_figures** — Two different Citizens assessment exposure figures appear on the site: the commercial residential page states a Citizens Policyholder Assessment of up to 15 percent, and the Citizens page states an assessment of up to 45 percent of premium. Both are Slide's characterisation of a third-party program, they do not agree with each other, and neither was verified against Citizens. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **depop_experience_claim** — Slide states its leadership team has participated in over 30 Citizens personal and commercial depopulation programs, that it maintains a $1 billion reinsurance program, and that its claims team achieved industry-leading results in hurricane response. These are undated company claims and outcome characterisations that were not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Workbook fit**

| Growth Through Service | primary | Elite Onboarding Experience | A Citizens takeout arrives as a book of households that did not choose you, each with a renewal decision and a coverage comparison waiting. That is an onboarding and service problem before it is a sales problem, and Slide publishes the comparison worksheets and the renewal mechanics the service team would work from. Who touches those households, in what order, is the agency's call. |
| Making It Rain | supporting_direct | The Opportunity Distribution | Depopulation assumptions and a coastal appetite are a distribution of opportunity that shows up without marketing spend. Supporting rather than primary because Slide supplies the market access, not the campaign, and the lane only exists in the states listed. |
| The Sales Sequence | supporting_direct | Position Definition & Focus % | Pre-filled quoting and fast underwriting responses shorten the quote step of a producer's call, and the endorsement list gives a producer concrete coverage choices to walk a client through. It supports a sequence that already exists; it does not write one. |
| The Operating System | conditional | Your Agency Bible | Conditional, and deliberately so: Slide currently spreads agent work across Violet, a legacy platform, a commercial platform and a single sign-on depending on state and product. That is exactly the kind of thing that has to be written down rather than remembered, so it argues for an Agency Bible entry rather than removing the need for one. |
| Commitment to Training | conditional | Welcome to the Team | Slide staffs an Agency Training and Communications Manager and publishes comparison material a team can be trained on. Conditional because no curriculum, scoring or completion evidence is published; the training standard and the gate stay with the agency. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Take one takeout renewal and write the exact service sequence: who calls, what gets compared, and what the client walks away knowing.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Assumed policies contacted before renewal, and retention at first Slide renewal.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Write down every source of opportunity you currently have that costs you nothing, and rank them by what you actually worked last month.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Quotes generated from assumed or takeout households versus purchased leads.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Time your current property quote from first question to presented options, then decide which step you would cut.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Minutes from data capture to a presented property quote.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Write the one-page rule for which system your team uses for which state and product, and name who keeps it current.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Whether a new hire can place business in the right system without asking.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Pick the one carrier workflow your team gets wrong most often and decide how someone proves they can run it.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Team members who can quote in the correct system unaided on the first attempt.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Agent platform routing: which system handles which state and product, across Violet and the legacy and commercial platforms.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves a new hire placing business in the right system without asking anyone.

**Open questions**

- Appointment requirements, production minimums, commission rates and contract terms. The agent page routes to an appointment request form and publishes none of these.
- Premium pricing, rate filings and any published rate comparison. Slide states its rates are competitive with Citizens but publishes no numbers.
- Underwriting appetite in detail: eligible construction, roof age, distance to coast, prior claims and other property guidelines are behind the agent login.
- Which of the six named states are actually open for new agent appointments today, and whether New York and New Jersey are writing or announced.
- Whether the South Carolina Violet rollout, listed as coming soon, has shipped.
- Any retention, close-rate, loss-ratio or agency production figure. None were published and none were asserted.
- The exact Citizens assessment exposure figure, given the site publishes two different percentages on two different pages.
- Whether Slide offers agency-level reporting, book transfer, marketing co-op or training beyond the named field team.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)

### 29. CRC Tapco

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://www.crctapco.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/crc-tapco.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `crc-tapco`
- **Category:** Wholesale insurance broker and managing general agency with binding authority for surplus lines personal and commercial property and casualty
- **Also known as:** Tapco, TAPCO Underwriters, TAPCO Underwriters, Inc., CRC Insurance Services, CRC Group
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (24 researched facts)**

- **what** — CRC Tapco is a wholesale insurance broker with binding authority, operating as a division of CRC Insurance Services, LLC. Its stated purpose is to give retail insurance agents fast access to specialty and surplus lines markets. _(public_company_claim)_
- **history** — The business began in 1983 as TAPCO Underwriters, a North Carolina based managing general agency, and was acquired by CRC Insurance Services in 2009, joining one of the largest wholesale brokers in the country. _(public_company_claim)_
- **classes** — CRC Tapco states it offers more than 1,000 general liability and property classes under its binding authority, spanning commercial general liability, personal lines, property and package, vacant property and builders risk, and artisan and specialty classes. A downloadable eligible class code list is published. _(public_company_claim)_
- **carriers** — CRC Tapco states it represents 20 or more domestic and alien carriers, all carrying A.M. Best ratings of A or better, and publishes a carrier partner list as a downloadable PDF. _(public_company_claim)_
- **service_promise** — CRC Tapco publishes a specific service standard: calls answered within 30 seconds, quotes provided within 5 minutes during the call, instant electronic quotes and binders, quick policy issuance once money and application are received, and quick endorsement processing. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_contract** — As a managing general agency, CRC Tapco states it does not require agency agreements, does not appoint agents, and does not involve an agent-company relationship. Placements are solely brokerage transactions. _(public_company_claim)_
- **licensing** — The license needed to place surplus lines business varies by state. CRC Tapco states most states require a valid Property and Casualty license plus some form of brokers license, and directs the agent to their state insurance department for specifics. _(public_company_claim)_
- **surplus_lines_filing** — CRC Tapco states it is surplus lines licensed in every state in which it writes and makes the surplus lines tax filings for all premium written through it, so the retail agent does not file those taxes. _(public_company_claim)_
- **state_forms** — Where a state requires a diligent search, declination affidavit or similar form, CRC Tapco attaches the form to the quote letter and binder invoice email and files it with the state department on the agent's behalf. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_broker_binding** — Retail brokers do not get binding authority. CRC Tapco states that as a managing general agency it is not allowed to give brokers binding authority and all coverage must be bound by a CRC Tapco underwriter. _(public_company_claim)_
- **quote_flow** — The stated workflow is to call an underwriter for a quote, with the majority of business quoted by phone and every quote emailed immediately. A binder is requested by phone prompt or by emailing TapcoBinders@crcgroup.com, and on binding CRC Tapco emails the required application, a completed finance agreement, any required state form and the binder invoice. Online quoting is also available at quoting.crctapco.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **binder_life** — Binders are good for 12 days from the effective date of coverage. Expired binders cannot be rebound, and applications and premium received after the binder period must be underwritten again to determine acceptability. _(public_company_claim)_
- **payment_terms** — All CRC Tapco business must be paid in full at inception. Most annual premiums can be financed through AFCO or IPFS, or through any finance company the insured chooses. Agency checks are preferred but insured checks are accepted, and VISA, Mastercard, American Express, Discover and ACH are taken. _(public_company_claim)_
- **billing** — CRC Tapco offers direct bill for personal lines renewals and options for commercial renewals, but states the majority of billing is on a cash basis, meaning net premium is submitted along with the application. It does direct bill insureds. _(public_company_claim)_
- **applications** — An application is required for every new account. Most business is submitted on ACORD or comparable forms, certain special programs need specific applications that CRC Tapco provides, and CRC Tapco publishes streamlined in-house applications. The appropriate application is emailed with every quote letter or binder invoice. _(public_company_claim)_
- **lines_written** — CRC Tapco states it writes Commercial Property Excess, Dwelling Fire, General Liability, Homeowners, Inland Marine, Liquor Liability, Personal Liability, Property, Umbrella, Vacant, Builders Risk and Wind Deductible Buy Back. The site navigation also lists Flood on both the personal and commercial side. _(public_company_claim)_
- **gl_appetite** — Named general liability programs are Artisan Contractor, Home Health Care, Churches and Houses of Worship (liability, property and clergy professional), and Landscape, Lawn Care and Tree Trimming or Removal. The GL page also names contractors, convenience stores and restaurants among hundreds of additional classes. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ho3** — The HO-3 homeowners product is Florida only, Coverage A from a $125K minimum to a $750K maximum, full updates required if the home is over 25 years old, water damage included with a $10K sublimit, wind and hail available subject to underwriting guidelines, replacement cost on the dwelling and actual cash value on personal property, quotable by phone or web. Monroe County is not eligible. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ho8** — The HO-8 homeowners product is available in AL, DC, FL, GA, KY, MD, NC, NJ, PA, SC, TN, TX and VA, Coverage A from a $50K minimum to a $500K maximum, no age restrictions, basic form perils at actual cash value, wind and hail available subject to guidelines, quotable by phone or web. Monroe County FL, Camden City and Camden County NJ, and Shelby County TN are not eligible. _(public_company_claim)_
- **claims** — CRC Tapco runs a full in-house claims staff. Claims are emailed to tapcoclaims@crctapco.com and reported to the North Carolina home office. The carrier involved plus the size and type of claim determine whether CRC Tapco adjusts in house or forwards it to the insurer. _(public_company_claim)_
- **certificates_and_delivery** — CRC Tapco discourages retail brokers from issuing certificates of insurance on behalf of its carriers, offering instead online COI issuance for informational purposes and certificate holder additions after issuance, with requests to tapcocois@crcgroup.com. Policies are delivered by email as PDF and by download through IVANS. _(public_company_claim)_
- **commission** — CRC Tapco states that commission paid to retail brokers varies with the product involved. No commission rate is published on the public site. _(public_company_claim)_
- **signup** — Agencies sign up through a three-step online Become a Broker form covering contact information, policy information and document upload, capturing agency name, phone, whether the agency is part of a group, physical and mailing addresses, and a primary contact name and email. _(public_company_claim)_
- **audience_restriction** — CRC Tapco states its material is intended for licensed insurance agency use only and not for business owner or insured use, and that nothing on the site constitutes an offer, inducement or contract of insurance. Financial strength and size ratings should be reevaluated before coverage is bound. _(public_company_claim)_

**Sources**

- CRC Tapco — Call. Quote. Bind. — https://www.crctapco.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- About Us — CRC Tapco — https://www.crctapco.com/About/About-Us (seen 2026-09-09)
- Frequently Asked Questions — CRC Tapco — https://www.crctapco.com/About/FAQs (seen 2026-09-09)
- Solutions for General Liability — CRC Tapco Commercial Lines — https://www.crctapco.com/Products/Commercial-Lines/Commercial-General-Liability (seen 2026-09-09)
- Solutions for Homeowners — CRC Tapco Personal Lines — https://www.crctapco.com/page/Homeowners (seen 2026-09-09)
- Become a Broker — CRC Tapco — https://www.crctapco.com/Broker-Services/Become-a-Broker (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Making It Rain | primary | The Opportunity Distribution | Most agencies leak revenue at the same point: a risk the standard market declines gets a shrug instead of a market. A binding-authority wholesaler with a published class list turns that pile into a distribution lane you can actually work. The decision about which declined risks are worth chasing, and who chases them, stays with the owner. |
| The Operating System | supporting_direct | Your Agency Bible | The mechanics here are unusually written down for a wholesaler: how a quote is requested, who is allowed to bind, that a binder dies at 12 days, that premium is due in full at inception. That is the raw text of a written internal procedure, and the 12-day binder in particular is the kind of deadline that only fails when nobody wrote it down. |
| The Sales Sequence | supporting_direct | Position Definition & Focus % | A quote inside a five-minute phone call means a producer can keep a hard-to-place risk inside the same conversation instead of promising a call back. That changes what a sales sequence can commit to. It does not define the seat, the focus percentages or the talk path. |
| Growth Through Service | conditional | Synchronicity | In-house claims, direct bill on personal lines renewals, tax and affidavit filing and IVANS download all remove service friction from surplus lines business. Conditional because the surplus-lines path adds its own service burden — cash-basis billing, restricted certificate issuance — that the service team has to absorb rather than avoid. |
| Commitment to Training | conditional | Assess the Training | There is real teachable content here — licensing requirements, what a binder is and is not, who may bind — and getting it wrong is a compliance problem rather than a sales problem. Conditional because CRC Tapco publishes reference material, not a course, and no scoring or completion evidence exists. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce anything new. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Count the risks you turned away or lost last month, sort them by class, and name the two classes worth building a market for.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Declined or non-standard risks submitted to a wholesale market per month, and how many bound.
- _The Operating System_ exercise: Write the surplus-lines submission procedure end to end, including who watches the binder clock and what happens on day 10.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Binders that expired without application and premium in the last 90 days.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Write what your producer says today when a risk does not fit the standard market, then write what you want them to say instead.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Non-standard risks quoted during the first call rather than deferred.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Name every extra step a surplus-lines policy creates for your service team, then decide which one you will systematise first.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Service touches per surplus-lines policy compared with a standard-market policy.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Ask each producer to state, without looking, who is allowed to bind coverage and how long a binder lasts.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Team members who can answer the binding and licensing questions correctly unaided.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: Surplus-lines submission mechanics: who requests the quote, who is allowed to bind, and the binder clock that runs out at twelve days.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves a deadline that only gets missed because the procedure was never written down.

**Open questions**

- Commission rates. CRC Tapco states commission varies by product and publishes no schedule.
- Premium pricing for any class. Quoting is by phone or behind a login and no plain public price exists.
- The identity of the 20-plus carrier partners and the eligible class code list. Both are published only as downloadable PDFs that were not opened.
- Which states each commercial program is available in. Only the homeowners products publish an explicit state list.
- Detailed underwriting guidelines beyond the homeowners limits and county exclusions published on the product pages.
- Turnaround on complex or larger accounts. The 30-second answer and 5-minute quote standard is stated for phone-quoted binding-authority business and was not verified.
- Whether the online quoting portal covers the same classes as the phone desk, or a narrower subset.
- Any bind ratio, loss ratio or agency production figure. None were published and none were asserted.

### 30. Ask Fetch

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://askfetch.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/ask-fetch.png`
- **Resource-page id:** `ask-fetch`
- **Category:** Insurance underwriting intelligence and home quoting software
- **Also known as:** Ask Fetch, Fetch, Fetch! Underwriting Made Easy
- **Research version:** 2026-08-31.2, reviewed 2026-08-31

**Podcast interview**

- Guest: Trace Meek
- Vimeo id: 1223988859
- Contact: Email Trace → mailto:trace@askfetch.com
- Shown as: trace@askfetch.com · askfetch.com
- Note: Trace invited agents to start a seven-day trial at Ask Fetch or email him directly for help getting started.

**What we know (24 researched facts)**

- **founders** — AskFetch names Trace Meek and Josh Mirabella as co-founders; it evolved from Fetch! Underwriting Made Easy. _(public_company_claim)_
- **company_scope** — The company serves independent insurance agents with carrier intelligence, structured risk matching, Ellie AI and quoting. It is a software partner, not an agency management coach. _(public_company_claim)_
- **policy_forms** — Its published home-policy workflow includes DP1, DP3, HO3, HO5 and HO8; actual carrier eligibility still requires confirmation. _(public_company_claim)_
- **location** — The website lists 2840 W Bay Drive, Suite 118, Belleair Bluffs, FL 33770. This is a published contact address, not independently verified headquarters. _(public_company_claim)_
- **scale** — The About page advertises 3,800+ active agents. This is an undated marketing count, not audited current adoption. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **ellie_workflow** — Ellie answers natural-language carrier eligibility questions across multiple carriers and explains acceptance, rejection or conditions. It is positioned for checking risk fit before quoting. _(public_company_claim)_
- **resource_library** — The product includes a carrier resource library and underwriting data. Marketplace combines risk matching with quoting; this does not establish a complete internal agency SOP or learning-management system. _(public_company_claim)_
- **unsupported_outcomes** — The Ellie page displays industry-research percentages about manual errors and claims time. They are not identified as measured AskFetch customer results; do not use them as AskFetch ROI evidence. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **geography** — The FAQ says full quoting is Florida-only; Ellie underwriting spans roughly 30 states. Confirm the attendee's state and appointed carriers before recommending a workflow. _(public_company_claim)_
- **maintenance** — AskFetch says carrier guidelines and bulletins are checked daily; exact carrier coverage and update service levels require confirmation. _(public_company_claim)_
- **ai_connectors** — The FAQ documents a Claude MCP connector, describes Gemini/Grok side-panel use, and marks ChatGPT support as forthcoming. This does not connect Formula to AskFetch's live underwriting data. _(public_company_claim)_
- **alongside_ams** — Ellie can be used alongside existing raters and AMS tools. Side-by-side use is not proof of a native data integration. _(public_company_claim)_
- **faq_feature_changes** — Search-index FAQ text describes EOS consumer links, email/PDF-to-quote, flood and future HO6. The opened FAQ omits that product group; obtain current written confirmation. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **security_marketing** — The FAQ claims encryption and role-based access. That is not independent certification or a reviewed AI data-processing agreement. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **published_pricing** — The pricing page displays $25 and $75 per seat per month and month-to-month terms. Feature markings differ from the Ellie page; confirm current packaging and any event offer before quoting a purchase price. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **feature_conflict** — Pricing marks Ellie Plus and Ellie Quoting as coming soon and EOS differently by tier; the Ellie page lists Ellie Quoting without that marker and EOS as forthcoming. Do not promise these features from this research. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **quote_stage** — AskFetch describes an approximately seven-second ranked indication followed by carrier-portal completion in about a minute. Treat these as vendor estimates for different stages, not a binding quote, coverage approval or guaranteed timing. _(public_company_claim)_
- **api_workflow** — AskFetch describes direct carrier APIs and standardized inputs followed by refinement in the carrier portal. Do not treat marketing assertions about stable rankings or universal coverage defaults as insurance advice. _(public_company_claim)_
- **bird_evidence** — An AskFetch-published Bird Insurance Group story attributes faster CSR quoting and more monthly policies to adoption. It supplies no baseline, controlled comparison or numeric uplift; use only as a named qualitative anecdote. _(public_company_claim)_
- **gravity_auth** — GravityCerts documents an AskFetch API-key connection using Quotes/Helpers permissions and an AskFetch admin email. This is provider documentation, not a tested Formula integration. Credentials belong in a secret store, never this knowledge record. _(integration_provider_documentation)_
- **gravity_intake** — GravityCerts documents website home-quote feeds, producer assignment, appointed-carrier filtering, mapped applicant/property fields and a Florida-only condition. This supports a conditional quote-intake use case, not promised lead volume or referral coaching. _(integration_provider_documentation)_
- **legal_identity** — The site's terms identify Fetch Holdings, LLC as the contracting entity and describe the product as informational quoting software, not an insurance carrier, underwriter or agent. _(public_company_claim)_
- **carrier_authority** — Carrier approval remains the authority for risk acceptance and binding. This company profile is not a live carrier guideline database; never answer a specific coverage or eligibility decision from it. _(public_company_claim)_
- **contact** — The privacy page publishes support@fetchuw.com and (727) 264-5530 for privacy questions. Verify the preferred partner/event contact instead of assuming this legacy address is the event representative. _(public_company_claim)_

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Operating System_ exercise: Design an answer-first workflow: lookup, record source/date, verify unresolved questions, escalate to a named role.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Underwriting questions resolved without owner interruption; unresolved exceptions reviewed.
- _Commitment to Training_ exercise: Draft a supervised practice set using synthetic property scenarios; require source checks and a documented escalation on uncertain answers.
- _Commitment to Training_ measure: Observed scenario accuracy and time to first independently verified quote; collect the baseline before setting a target.
- _The Sales Sequence_ exercise: Identify one preventable quoting interruption and design the AskFetch-owned product, onboarding or support change that removes it.
- _The Sales Sequence_ measure: Time to first usable indication, failed quote handoffs and verified time returned to producers.
- _Growth Through Service_ exercise: Specify a handoff containing the risk question, current source, open condition, next owner and confirmation status.
- _Growth Through Service_ measure: Handoffs returned for missing underwriting information and repeat clarification requests.
- _Making It Rain_ exercise: Map one existing Florida home lead source through intake, producer assignment and follow-up; validate the actual integration before printing a claim.
- _Making It Rain_ measure: Intake completion and source-to-quote conversion, with the agency's own baseline.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: AI-assisted carrier matching, risk assessment and underwriting workflows.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves getting underwriting knowledge out of one person's head and shortening a repeatable quoting process.

**Open questions**

- Actual Formula partnerOrgId, authenticated representative UID and verified membership. The designated contact is recorded separately; no account or organization was found at the last check.
- AskFetch's internal org chart, assigned owners, capacity, budget, baseline KPIs, customer metrics and 2027 targets.
- The partner's confirmed One Company Domino and in-room observations; public research cannot supply them.
- Current state/carrier list, line-of-business matrix, appointment requirements and connector entitlements.
- Current EOS, email-to-quote, Ellie Quoting, Ellie Plus, flood, HO6 and ChatGPT availability; pages conflict or describe roadmap.
- Current pricing, trial terms, event discount, booking URL and approved logo/print copy.
- Permission and technical contract for a live AskFetch API/MCP connection, including data use, retention, deletion, AI training and subprocessors.
- Independently substantiated quantitative case studies or security certification evidence.

### 31. Elite Travel Hackers

- **Tier:** Bronze
- **Website:** https://elitetravelhackers.com
- **Logo:** `/assets/sponsors/elite-travel-hackers.svg`
- **Resource-page id:** `elite-travel-hackers`
- **Category:** Points and miles consulting and concierge travel booking for business owners and executives
- **Also known as:** Elite Travel & Content, Elite Travel & Content Inc, Elite Points & Miles, Counting Countries
- **Research version:** 2026-09-09.1, reviewed 2026-09-09

**What we know (24 researched facts)**

- **entity** — Elite Travel Hackers is a program operated by Elite Travel & Content (shown as Elite Travel & Content Inc on the application page). The Terms and the Privacy Policy both carry an effective date of October 14, 2024. _(public_company_claim)_
- **audience** — The stated audience is business owners and executives. The pitch is that a business already spending tens of thousands a month on payroll, office supplies and client dinners is earning too few points on that spend and redeeming poorly. _(public_company_claim)_
- **core_offer** — The core offer is to turn everyday personal and business expenses into premium travel using points and miles, described as first-class flights and five-star hotel stays booked for a fraction of the cash price. _(public_company_claim)_
- **delivery_model** — Delivery is a done-for-you service, not a course alone. The published four steps are: analyse business and personal expenses to find missed earning; build a personalised points-earning and redemption plan; unlock more value from existing points; and handle the search, booking and optimisation so the client gets the flights, hotel upgrades, lounge access and status benefits. _(public_company_claim)_
- **concierge_booking** — The FAQ states there is a concierge booking service that finds deals, books the flights and hotels, and optimises redemptions on the client's behalf. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_spend_change** — The company states clients do not need to change their spending habits; the work is optimising how points are earned and redeemed on spend that is already happening. _(public_company_claim)_
- **card_strategy** — The stated method includes optimising the client's credit card strategy and using transfer partners rather than cashing points out, with Amex and Chase points named as examples of currencies clients commonly redeem badly. _(public_company_claim)_
- **intake_gate** — The front door is a qualification application, not an open booking link. The form asks for name, email, phone, whether the applicant is based in the US or Canada, credit score, average total monthly credit card spend across business and personal, current total points and miles balance, and the applicant's biggest rewards challenge. _(public_company_claim)_
- **us_canada** — US or Canada residency is asked as a qualifying question on the application, so the served market is North America. _(public_company_claim)_
- **entry_point** — Every call to action on the site leads to a free strategy or discovery call, scheduled through a Calendly link or through the application form. There is no self-serve purchase path on the pages observed. _(public_company_claim)_
- **founder** — The named public face is Jordan Egbert, known on social media as Counting Countries, described as having discovered travel hacking six years ago after leaving a door-to-door sales job. _(public_company_claim)_
- **program_contents** — The Terms list the services as access to the Elite Travel Hackers community, educational courses and workshops on travel hacking, points and miles, travel hacking tools and resources including deal alerts and mistake fares, downloadable cheat sheets, templates and guides, and eligibility for giveaways and special offers. _(public_company_claim)_
- **family_group** — The company states it handles family and group travel strategies, so bookings are not limited to a single traveller, and that bookable redemptions include first-class and business-class airfare, luxury hotels, premium resorts, rental cars and experiences. _(public_company_claim)_
- **terms_basics** — Published contract terms: users must be at least 18, all fees are listed in U.S. dollars unless otherwise specified, the company may modify the Terms at any time without prior notice, and content is licensed for personal non-commercial use only. _(public_company_claim)_
- **refunds** — The published refund policy is that because the content and services are digital the company generally does not offer refunds, and considers requests case by case at the support team's discretion. Refund inquiries go to support@elitetravelhackers.com. _(public_company_claim)_
- **data_handling** — The Privacy Policy states payment details are handled by third-party payment processors and are not stored on the company's servers, and that contact, account and usage data are collected across the website, waitlist and newsletter. _(public_company_claim)_
- **skool_membership** — A membership community named Elite Points & Miles, run by Jordan Egbert and reached from the Elite Travel Hackers site, is published on Skool at a plain public price of $79 per month, cancel anytime. It is a private community listing a training library, weekly live workshops, monthly Q&A, deal alerts and airline and hotel cheat sheets. _(public_company_claim)_
- **no_carrier_endorsement** — The site footer disclaims any endorsement by or affiliation with Meta, Facebook, TikTok, Google or YouTube, and states that all views belong to Elite Travel & Content and the Elite Travel Hackers program. _(public_company_claim)_
- **founder_result_claims** — The founder biography claims over $1.2 million saved in travel expenses, 150-plus countries visited, 500-plus flights and hotel stays, and first-class travel on Emirates, Singapore Airlines and Qatar Airways. These are undated personal claims published by the company and were not independently verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **client_outcome_claims** — The site claims all clients unlock a minimum of $20,000 to $50,000 in free travel annually while saving up to 97 percent off the cash price, that clients earn 2-3x more points, redeem for 5-10x more value, and unlock 500 percent more value from their points. These are vendor outcome claims with no published methodology or sample. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **loss_framing_claims** — The site asserts that a typical prospect is leaving $75,000 to $150,000 or more in travel value unused every year and headlines $50,000 vacations for less than the price of economy tickets. These are marketing framings, not measured figures. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **testimonial_claims** — Named testimonials on the home page claim outcomes including a $15,000 Emirates first-class ticket booked for under $1,400, over one million points and $100,000-plus in free travel earned in a first 30 days, and a Maldives honeymoon booked entirely on points. These are customer statements published by the vendor and were not verified. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **skool_secondary_claims** — The Skool listing stacks a $12,479 total value against the $79 monthly price, shows 16 members, and advertises tiered launch pricing rising to $99 and then $119 per month after a November 3 date with no year stated. The value stack and the launch tiers are undated marketing and were not confirmed. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_
- **second_principal** — The scheduling link on the site is a Calendly under the handle justin-elitetravelhackers, indicating a second principal named Justin who is not named anywhere in the site copy. A third-party podcast listing names Justin Froeber and Jordan Egbert as running Elite Travel Hackers, but that was not confirmed on any company page. _(needs_confirmation, not used in AI)_

**Sources**

- Elite Travel Hackers - Home — https://elitetravelhackers.com/ (seen 2026-09-09)
- Elite Travel Hackers - Executive Concierge Application — https://elitetravelhackers.com/executiveconciergeapp (seen 2026-09-09)
- Elite Travel & Content and Elite Travel Hackers Terms & Conditions — https://www.elitetravelhackers.com/terms (seen 2026-09-09)
- Elite Travel & Content and Elite Travel Hackers Privacy Policy — https://www.elitetravelhackers.com/privacypolicy (seen 2026-09-09)
- Elite Points & Miles - Skool community by Jordan Egbert — https://www.skool.com/elitepointsandmiles/about (seen 2026-09-09)

**Workbook fit**

| Session | Fit | Where it lands | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| The Operating System | conditional | Your Agency Bible | How an agency pays for things is an operating-system decision, and routing existing spend through a deliberate card strategy is a written standard like any other. Conditional and narrow: this touches the payment rail only. It does not touch how the agency runs, who leads, or what the standard is for anything else, and an owner should treat it as a finance-side policy rather than an operating change. |
| The Sales Sequence | not_recommended | — | Nothing published by this partner touches the producer seat, the call, recruiting or accountability. Placing a travel rewards vendor in the sales session would be an invention. |
| Growth Through Service | not_recommended | — | The published offering is points optimisation and travel booking for the owner. It has no service, onboarding or retention capability for an agency's customers. |
| Commitment to Training | not_recommended | — | The company does publish courses, workshops and a membership library, but they teach travel hacking to the owner, not agency skills to a team. Nothing here trains a producer or a service rep. |
| Making It Rain | not_recommended | — | Marketing spend earning more points is a rebate on cost, not a marketing strategy. The partner produces no leads, no creative and no channel work, and should not be placed in this session. |
| The Body Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Being Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. |
| The Balance Session | not_recommended | — | Company research is deliberately excluded from the personal workbooks at runtime. Family travel is personal territory and a vendor has no place shaping it. |
| My 2027 Map | reference_only | — | The Map compiles decisions already made in the other sessions. Company research is not loaded here and should not introduce a vendor decision that was not made earlier. |

**Room exercises and measures**

- _The Operating System_ exercise: List your five largest recurring business expenses and write down which card each one currently runs on and why.
- _The Operating System_ measure: Share of monthly business spend running on a deliberately chosen card versus on whatever was set up first.

**On these resource pages**

- **Operating System Resources** (/resources/operating-system)
  - Helps with: A deliberate card and points strategy for the business spend you are already making. Finance-side policy, not an operating change.
  - Best fit: Your Domino involves deciding which card each recurring expense runs on and why, rather than living with whatever was set up first.

**Open questions**

- The price of the consulting and concierge service. The FAQ says pricing depends on your needs and directs prospects to a free consultation, so it is gated and is deliberately absent here. Only the $79 per month Skool community price is published as a plain public price.
- The engagement model behind the concierge service: whether it is a one-time plan, a retainer, a per-booking fee, a commission on redemption value, or a mix.
- Whether the company is compensated by credit card issuers through referral or affiliate arrangements. The 2024 footer on one page reads Affiliate Content Creator, and the site recommends card strategy, so a compensation conflict is possible and is not disclosed on the pages observed.
- The legal entity's state of incorporation, registered address and any licensing. Only the name Elite Travel & Content Inc appears, with no address published on the pages observed.
- Whether Justin Froeber is a principal. The site names only Jordan Egbert; the Calendly handle implies a second principal but no company page names him.
- Minimum qualifying monthly card spend, credit score or points balance. The application asks for all three but publishes no threshold.
- Any verified client outcome. Every savings, multiple and percentage figure on the site is an unmethodised vendor claim and is held out of AI context here.
- Whether the offering is tailored in any way to insurance agencies specifically, or is generic to business owners.
- The relationship between the Elite Travel Hackers program, the Elite Points & Miles Skool community, and the executive concierge service - the site links them without explaining how they fit together.
- Elite Travel Hackers' own org chart, capacity, assigned Formula contact and 2027 targets.

**Activation notes**

- runtimeSurface: Partner-seat business workbooks only (s1, s2, s4, s5, s7)
- identityStatus: no_representative_checked
- disclosure: Research compiled from five public pages observed on 2026-09-09: four on elitetravelhackers.com plus the linked Skool community listing. Identity was confirmed by matching the brand name, the business-owner audience, the named principal Jordan Egbert of Counting Countries, and a Calendly handle under the elitetravelhackers domain. No contact with the company was made.
- note: Heavy outcome marketing. Every savings, multiple and percentage claim is marked needs_confirmation and excluded from AI context. Possible undisclosed card-issuer affiliate compensation is listed as an unknown and should be resolved with the partner before this record is presented to members.
